### 🤖 Claude · 2026-10-11 06:51:15

Mostly it's the preview, plus a real difference underneath:

- **The preview:** v12 shows the phone frames at 66% scale, while v11's were 1:1.
- **The real difference:** v11 mocked a narrow ~320 px phone. v12 renders a real 390 px iPhone at the same font sizes, so everything takes up a smaller share of the screen.

v13 fixes both. Text and elements now scale with screen width, so any phone keeps v11's proportions, and the previews are shown at v11's size. Tablet gets the same treatment. On top of that:
- tablet: cookie card and calculator centred
- desktop: a full-width cookie bar with centred buttons
- tablet and desktop: "Kolik investovat a na jak dlouho?"

**→ Write** `C:\Users\matus\Desktop\B_project\scripts\inline_v12_preview.py`

```
"""Inline a site file and the e-mail into a device-showcase page (v12, v13, ...).

The showcase renders the site and the e-mail in iframes via `srcdoc`, so it also works
when the page is viewed as a single file/snapshot. Re-run after editing a source file:

  python -I scripts/inline_v12_preview.py design/previews            # v12
  python -I scripts/inline_v12_preview.py design/previews 13         # v13 (uses v12-email.html)
"""
import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def js_string(text):
    # JSON-encode and escape "</" so the payload cannot close the <script> tag early.
    return json.dumps(text).replace("</", "<\\/")


def main(folder, version="12"):
    d = Path(folder)
    site = (d / f"v{version}-site.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    email_file = d / f"v{version}-email.html"
    email = (email_file if email_file.exists() else d / "v12-email.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    show = d / f"v{version}.html"
    page = show.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    payload = "window.SITE=" + js_string(site) + ";window.EMAIL=" + js_string(email) + ";"
    page, n = re.subn(r'<script id="inline">.*?</script>', lambda m: '<script id="inline">' + payload + "</script>", page, count=1, flags=re.S)
    if n != 1:
        sys.exit(f"inline slot not found in {show.name}")
    show.write_text(page, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"inlined site ({len(site)} chars) and e-mail ({len(email)} chars) into {show}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "design/previews", sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "12")
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-10-11 06:53:58

v13 has all four fixes, and I checked each one with measurements and screenshots ([design/previews/v13.html](design/previews/v13.html), commit `2c21b29`).

**Mobile looking smaller:** it was two things together.
- The v12 preview showed the phones at 66% size, while v11 showed them full size.
- More importantly, v11 mocked a narrow ~320 px phone, and v12 rendered a real 390 px iPhone at the same font sizes, so everything took up a smaller share of the screen.

Text and elements now grow with the screen width, so any phone looks like v11: the headline is about 48 px on a 390 px phone, with larger checks, chips, buttons and bars to match. The previews are also shown close to v11's size now.

**Tablet**
- Everything is scaled up: headline about 77 px, larger checks, 60 px chips and a 64 px CTA.
- The calculator is centred.
- The cookie card is centred too. While it's showing, the hero moves up so the CTA isn't covered.

**Desktop:** the cookie consent is now a full-width bar at the bottom. The text is centred, "Odmítnout" and "Přijmout" are the same width with "Přijmout" in lime, and "Nastavení" sits below. On a 900 px tall screen it doesn't cover the calculator.

**Heading:** tablet and desktop now say "Kolik investovat a na jak dlouho?". Mobile keeps "Kolik a na jak dlouho?" because the narrow card would push it to three lines; say if you want the longer one there too.

What's next: more design changes, or shall I write the build plan?

# Build plan: Veenos landing page (Zadanie B)

## Context
The research, verification and design phases are finished. Every decision is logged in `research/decisions.md` (rounds 1–13). The approved design is the clickable responsive prototype `design/previews/v13-site.html`, which has variants A/B, the calculator, the 4-fund bar comparison, the form with the "Chci pomoct" phone opt-in and the cookie UI. The e-mail design is `design/previews/v12-email.html`.

Now we turn the prototype into a production page. It goes on Vercel (free), with a working lead form, real e-mail delivery, cookie-free full-funnel measurement, two ad creatives and a Slovak README that meets every requirement of the assignment. Deadline **16. 10. 2026**; today is 11. 10.

Choices confirmed for this phase:
- e-mail: **Brevo**, sending from a verified address you own, no domain needed
- storage: **Upstash Redis via Vercel Marketplace**, EU region
- analytics: **no GA4 or Meta Pixel**, own funnel log only
- README: **in Slovak**

Design tweaks can follow after the build.

## Architecture (no framework: static page + Vercel Node functions)
```
public/index.html          ← v13-site.html: real <form>, labels, a11y, no preview presets
public/js/model.js         ← fund data + cost model (ES module), the single source of truth
public/js/app.js           ← UI, A/B assignment, funnel beacons, form submit
public/fonts/*.woff2       ← self-hosted Manrope (latin + latin-ext); no Google Fonts call
public/navod.html          ← neutral guide "Jak koupit ETF u brokera" (linked from the e-mail)
public/ochrana-udaju.html  ← privacy policy + cookies section (operator details = clearly marked TODO)
public/og.png              ← social share image
api/lead.js                ← POST: validate, honeypot + min fill time, rate limit, store, send e-mails
api/event.js               ← POST (sendBeacon): whitelisted funnel events → Redis counters
api/stats.js               ← GET ?token=…: funnel per day / variant / utm_content (JSON + tiny HTML view)
lib/store.js               ← Upstash Redis; in-memory fallback when env vars are missing (local/dev)
lib/mailer.js              ← Brevo transactional API via fetch; console fallback when there's no key
lib/email-template.js      ← v12-email.html as a function (numbers from model.js; help block conditional; plain-text part)
lib/validate.js            ← input schema (allowed m/y values, e-mail, optional CZ phone)
scripts/dev-server.js      ← local static + /api router (no `vercel dev`/login needed for testing)
tests/*.test.js            ← node:test (model, validation, lead handler with mocks, template)
ads/ad-a.html, ad-b.html   ← creatives → PNG 1080×1350 (feed) + 1080×1920 (story) via headless Edge
ads/README.md              ← ad copy (primary text, headline, CTA, UTM destination) per version
vercel.json, package.json, .env.example, README.md (SK)
```
Reused as-is: `scripts/export_ai_log.py` (ai-log hook). The cost model, data and UI are ported from `design/previews/v13-site.html` and `v11.html` (`fv`/`compare`, buying cost per route only).

## Key implementation decisions
- **Cost model (`public/js/model.js`)**:
  - fund data comes from the verified facts: TER SPY 0.0945 %, IVV/VOO 0.03 %, CSPX 0.07 %
  - Fio: USD 7.95 per order, ≈3 % FX, bought quarterly
  - XTB: 0 %, 0.5 % FX
  - ČNB USD rate 21.745 (9. 10. 2026); 7 % p.a. before costs
  - each data point carries its source URL and as-of date
  - the API recomputes all numbers server-side, so client values are never trusted
  - tests lock in 3 000 Kč/20 y = CSPX 27 900, IVV/VOO 131 300, SPY 142 100
- **A/B**:
  - assignment is `?v=a|b` if present, otherwise random 50/50 per page load
  - nothing is stored on the device, so no consent is needed; a reload may switch variant, which is documented
  - A = Prague Castle + "Začít investovat"; B = young man + "Spočítat poplatky"
- **Funnel events (cookie-free)**:
  - events: `page_view`, `hero_cta`, `calc_select`, `calc_compare`, `result_view`, `f
… [truncated, 4974 more chars]
````

```
// Copies self-hosted Manrope (OFL) from @fontsource into public/fonts and writes public/fonts/manrope.css.
// Self-hosting avoids sending visitors' IPs to Google Fonts (GDPR) and saves a third-party connection.
import { copyFileSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

const SRC = 'node_modules/@fontsource/manrope/files';
const OUT = 'public/fonts';
const WEIGHTS = [400, 600, 700, 800];
const SUBSETS = {
  latin: 'U+0000-00FF,U+0131,U+0152-0153,U+02BB-02BC,U+02C6,U+02DA,U+02DC,U+0304,U+0308,U+0329,U+2000-206F,U+20AC,U+2122,U+2191,U+2193,U+2212,U+2215,U+FEFF,U+FFFD',
  'latin-ext': 'U+0100-02BA,U+02BD-02C5,U+02C7-02CC,U+02CE-02D7,U+02DD-02FF,U+0304,U+0308,U+0329,U+1D00-1DBF,U+1E00-1E9F,U+1EF2-1EFF,U+2020,U+20A0-20AB,U+20AD-20C0,U+2113,U+2C60-2C7F,U+A720-A7FF',
};

mkdirSync(OUT, { recursive: true });
let css = '/* Manrope (SIL Open Font License), self-hosted via @fontsource/manrope */\n';
for (const w of WEIGHTS) {
  for (const [subset, range] of Object.entries(SUBSETS)) {
    const file = `manrope-${subset}-${w}-normal.woff2`;
    copyFileSync(join(SRC, file), join(OUT, file));
    css += `@font-face{font-family:'Manrope';font-style:normal;font-display:swap;font-weight:${w};src:url(/fonts/${file}) format('woff2');unicode-range:${range}}\n`;
  }
}
writeFileSync(join(OUT, 'manrope.css'), css);
console.log(`copied ${WEIGHTS.length * 2} font files to ${OUT}`);
```

**→ Write** `C:\Users\matus\Desktop\B_project\scripts\dev-server.js`

```
// Local dev server: serves public/ and routes /api/<name> to api/<name>.js (Vercel-style handlers).
// No Vercel login needed. Without Upstash/Brevo env vars the API uses an in-memory store and a console mailer.
import { createServer } from 'node:http';
import { readFile, stat } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { extname, join, normalize } from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

// load .env.local if present (simple KEY=VALUE lines)
if (existsSync('.env.local')) {
  for (const line of readFileSync('.env.local', 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const m = line.match(/^\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*([^#]*?)\s*(#.*)?$/);
    if (m && m[2] && !process.env[m[1]]) process.env[m[1]] = m[2];
  }
}

const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT || 3000);
const ROOT = 'public';
const TYPES = { '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '.js': 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8', '.css': 'text/css; charset=utf-8', '.json': 'application/json', '.woff2': 'font/woff2', '.png': 'image/png', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.ico': 'image/x-icon', '.webp': 'image/webp' };

// minimal Vercel-like req/res helpers
function enhance(req, res, body, url) {
  req.query = Object.fromEntries(url.searchParams);
  req.body = body;
  res.status = code => { res.statusCode = code; return res; };
  res.json = obj => { res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json'); res.end(JSON.stringify(obj)); return res; };
  res.send = txt => { res.end(txt); return res; };
}

createServer(async (req, res) => {
  const url = new URL(req.url, `http://localhost:${PORT}`);
  try {
    if (url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) {
      const name = url.pathname.slice(5).replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/gi, '');
      const file = join('api', name + '.js');
      if (!existsSync(file)) { res.statusCode = 404; return res.end('not found'); }
      let raw = '';
      for await (const chunk of req) raw += chunk;
      let body = raw;
      try { body = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {}; } catch { /* keep text */ }
      enhance(req, res, body, url);
      const mod = await import(pathToFileURL(file).href + '?t=' + Date.now());
      return await mod.default(req, res);
    }
    let path = normalize(join(ROOT, decodeURIComponent(url.pathname)));
    if (!path.startsWith(ROOT)) { res.statusCode = 403; return res.end(); }
    const s = await stat(path).catch(() => null);
    if (s?.isDirectory()) path = join(path, 'index.html');
    else if (!s && existsSync(path + '.html')) path += '.html';   // clean URLs like Vercel
    const data = await readFile(path);
    res.setHeader('Content-Type', TYPES[extname(path)] || 'application/octet-stream');
    res.end(data);
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.code === 'ENOENT') { res.statusCode = 404; return res.end('not found'); }
    console.error(e); res.statusCode = 500; res.end('server error');
  }
}).listen(PORT, () => console.log(`Veenos dev server: http://localhost:${PORT}`));
```

// Veenos cost model: the single source of truth for the page, the API (e-mail numbers) and the tests.
// Every data point has a source and an as-of date; facts were verified in research/05-data-facts.md
// and research/99-verification-log.md. Values are illustrative inputs, not investment advice.

export const DATA_AS_OF = '2026-10-09';

export const ASSUMPTIONS = {
  annualReturn: 0.07, // 7 % p.a. before costs (illustrative, same for all funds: they track the same index)
  usdCzk: 21.745,     // ČNB rate for 9 Oct 2026
  usdCzkSource: 'https://api.cnb.cz/cnbapi/exrates/daily?lang=EN',
};

// How a Czech retail investor can buy each fund (research/05: PRIIPs blocks US-domiciled ETFs at most brokers; Fio allows them).
export const ROUTES = {
  fio: {
    name: 'Fio e-Broker',
    commissionUsd: 7.95,   // per order up to 100 shares (US market)
    fx: 0.03,              // CZK↔USD spread vs ČNB, indicative (undated table; e-Broker rates may differ)
    ordersEveryMonths: 3,  // assumption: NYSE funds bought once a quarter (fairer than monthly USD 7.95 orders)
    sources: ['https://www.fio.cz/docs/cz/C_zaklad.pdf', 'https://www.fio.cz/akcie-investice/dalsi-sluzby-fio/devizove-konverze'],
    asOf: '2026-07-01',
  },
  xtb: {
    name: 'XTB',
    commissionUsd: 0,      // 0 % commission on stocks/ETFs up to EUR 100 000 monthly turnover
    fx: 0.005,             // 0.5 % currency conversion
    ordersEveryMonths: 1,
    sources: ['https://www.xtb.com/cz/soubory/tabulka-poplatku-a-provizi.pdf'],
    asOf: '2026-04-29',
  },
};

export const FUNDS = [
  { ticker: 'SPY', name: 'SPDR S&P 500 ETF Trust', issuer: 'State Street', listing: 'NYSE Arca', domicile: 'USA', ter: 0.000945, route: 'fio',
    source: 'https://www.ssga.com/us/en/intermediary/etfs/spdr-sp-500-etf-trust-spy', asOf: '2026-10-08' },
  { ticker: 'IVV', name: 'iShares Core S&P 500 ETF', issuer: 'BlackRock (iShares)', listing: 'NYSE Arca', domicile: 'USA', ter: 0.0003, route: 'fio',
    source: 'https://www.ishares.com/us/products/239726/ishares-core-sp-500-etf', asOf: '2026-10-09' },
  { ticker: 'VOO', name: 'Vanguard S&P 500 ETF', issuer: 'Vanguard', listing: 'NYSE Arca', domicile: 'USA', ter: 0.0003, route: 'fio',
    source: 'https://investor.vanguard.com/investment-products/etfs/profile/voo', asOf: '2026-04-28' },
  { ticker: 'CSPX', name: 'iShares Core S&P 500 UCITS ETF (Acc)', issuer: 'BlackRock (iShares)', listing: 'Londýn / Xetra (SXR8)', domicile: 'Irsko', ter: 0.0007, route: 'xtb',
    isin: 'IE00B5BMR087', source: 'https://www.justetf.com/en/etf-profile.html?isin=IE00B5BMR087', asOf: '2026-10-09' },
];

export const LISTING_SOURCE = 'https://www.nasdaqtrader.com/dynamic/SymDir/otherlisted.txt';

export const ALLOWED = { monthly: [1000, 3000, 5000, 10000], years: [5, 10, 20, 30] };

const monthlyRate = r => Math.pow(1 + r, 1 / 12) - 1;

// Future value of a monthly plan. Costs: TER (yearly, charged monthly), FX on every purchase and on the final sale,
// a fixed commission per order; contributions wait in cash until the next order.
export function futureValue(monthly, years, { ter = 0, fx = 0, commissionCzk = 0, ordersEveryMonths = 1 } = {}, annualReturn = ASSUMPTIONS.annualReturn) {
  const r = monthlyRate(annualReturn);
  let value = 0, cash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < years * 12; i++) {
    value = value * (1 + r) * (1 - ter / 12);
    cash += monthly;
    if ((i + 1) % ordersEveryMonths === 0) { value += cash * (1 - fx) - commissionCzk; cash = 0; }
  }
  return (value + cash) * (1 - fx);
}

function routeParams(fund) {
  const r = ROUTES[fund.route];
  return { fx: r.fx, commissionCzk: r.commissionUsd * ASSUMPTIONS.usdCzk, ordersEveryMonths: r.ordersEveryMonths };
}

// Total costs in CZK per fund, cheapest first.
// buyCost depends only on the route (identical for SPY/IVV/VOO); feeCost is the rest of the total.
export function compare(monthly, years) {
  const base = futureValue(monthly, years);
  return FUNDS.map(f => {

```
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { compare, futureValue, roundCzk, isAllowed, ALLOWED, FUNDS } from '../public/js/model.js';

const byTicker = rows => Object.fromEntries(rows.map(r => [r.ticker, r]));

test('3 000 Kč a month for 20 years matches the approved design numbers', () => {
  const r = byTicker(compare(3000, 20));
  assert.equal(roundCzk(r.CSPX.total), 27900);
  assert.equal(roundCzk(r.IVV.total), 131300);
  assert.equal(roundCzk(r.VOO.total), 131300);
  assert.equal(roundCzk(r.SPY.total), 142100);
});

test('cheapest first; CSPX wins for a Czech investor in every allowed scenario', () => {
  for (const m of ALLOWED.monthly) for (const y of ALLOWED.years) {
    const rows = compare(m, y);
    for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) assert.ok(rows[i - 1].total <= rows[i].total);
    assert.equal(rows[0].ticker, 'CSPX', `${m}/${y}`);
  }
});

test('buying cost depends only on the route; fee cost follows TER', () => {
  const r = byTicker(compare(3000, 20));
  assert.equal(roundCzk(r.SPY.buyCost), roundCzk(r.IVV.buyCost));
  assert.equal(roundCzk(r.IVV.buyCost), roundCzk(r.VOO.buyCost));
  assert.ok(r.SPY.feeCost > r.IVV.feeCost);
  for (const row of Object.values(r)) assert.ok(Math.abs(row.feeCost + row.buyCost - row.total) < 1e-6);
});

test('no costs = no difference; costs never increase the value', () => {
  assert.equal(futureValue(3000, 20), futureValue(3000, 20, {}));
  assert.ok(futureValue(3000, 20, { ter: 0.001 }) < futureValue(3000, 20));
  assert.ok(futureValue(3000, 20, { fx: 0.01 }) < futureValue(3000, 20));
});

test('input whitelist and data completeness', () => {
  assert.ok(isAllowed(3000, 20));
  assert.ok(!isAllowed(2999, 20));
  assert.ok(!isAllowed(3000, 21));
  for (const f of FUNDS) { assert.ok(f.source.startsWith('https://')); assert.match(f.asOf, /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/); }
});
```

// A/B variant, decided before first paint (no flicker) and kept in memory only: nothing is stored on the device.
// ?v=a or ?v=b forces a variant (used by ad links and QA); otherwise 50/50 on each page load.
(function () {
  var q = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('v');
  var v = q === 'a' || q === 'b' ? q : (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'a' : 'b');
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-v', v);
})();
```

import { compare, formatCzk, formatPct, isAllowed, FUNDS, ROUTES, ASSUMPTIONS, DATA_AS_OF, LISTING_SOURCE } from './model.js';
import { TRACKERS } from './config.js';

const $ = (s, el = document) => el.querySelector(s);
const $$ = (s, el = document) => [...el.querySelectorAll(s)];
const B = document.body;
const VARIANT = document.documentElement.dataset.v || 'a';
const desktop = () => matchMedia('(min-width: 1100px)').matches;

/* ---------- funnel tracking: cookie-free, nothing stored on the device ---------- */
const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
const UTM = Object.fromEntries(['utm_source', 'utm_medium', 'utm_campaign', 'utm_content'].map(k => [k, (params.get(k) || '').slice(0, 60)]));
const PID = (crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID() : String(Math.random()).slice(2)); // per page load, in memory only
const DEVICE = innerWidth < 700 ? 'mobile' : innerWidth < 1100 ? 'tablet' : 'desktop';
const sent = new Set();
function track(e, extra = {}, once = true) {
  if (once && sent.has(e)) return;
  sent.add(e);
  const body = JSON.stringify({ e, v: VARIANT, pid: PID, d: DEVICE, ...UTM, ...extra });
  try {
    if (!(navigator.sendBeacon && navigator.sendBeacon('/api/event', body))) {
      fetch('/api/event', { method: 'POST', body, keepalive: true, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain' } }).catch(() => {});
    }
  } catch { /* tracking must never break the page */ }
}
track('page_view');

addEventListener('scroll', () => B.classList.toggle('scrolled', scrollY > 30), { passive: true });

/* ---------- dialogs (menu, form, cookie settings): focus trap + Esc ---------- */
let lastFocus = null;
const openDialogs = [];
function openDialog(el, cls) {
  lastFocus = document.activeElement;
  B.classList.add(cls);
  openDialogs.push({ el, cls });
  setTimeout(() => (el.querySelector('input:not([type=checkbox]):not([tabindex="-1"]), button, a') || el).focus(), 60);
}
function closeDialog(cls) {
  B.classList.remove(cls);
  const i = openDialogs.findIndex(d => d.cls === cls);
  if (i > -1) openDialogs.splice(i, 1);
  if (lastFocus) lastFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true });
}
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  const top = openDialogs[openDialogs.length - 1];
  if (!top) return;
  if (e.key === 'Escape') { closeDialog(top.cls); if (top.cls === 'menu-open') $('.js-menu').setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); }
  if (e.key === 'Tab') {
    const f = $$('a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([type=hidden]):not([tabindex="-1"])', top.el).filter(x => x.offsetParent !== null);
    if (!f.length) return;
    if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === f[0]) { e.preventDefault(); f[f.length - 1].focus(); }
    else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === f[f.length - 1]) { e.preventDefault(); f[0].focus(); }
  }
});

/* ---------- menu ---------- */
const menuBtn = $('.js-menu');
menuBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { menuBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true'); openDialog($('#menu'), 'menu-open'); });
$('.js-mclose').addEventListener('click', () => { menuBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); closeDialog('menu-open'); });
$$('.js-mlink').forEach(a => a.addEventListener('click', () => { menuBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); B.classList.remove('menu-open'); openDialogs.length = 0; }));

/* ---------- calculator ---------- */
let monthly = null, years = null;

}

$$('.js-y .chip').forEach(c => c.addEventListener('click', () => { years = Number(c.dataset.v); pick('.js-y', years); goBtn.disabled = !(monthly && years); track('calc_select'); }));

```
// Storage for funnel counters and leads. Upstash Redis in production (Vercel Marketplace integration),
// an in-memory store locally when no credentials are set.
import { Redis } from '@upstash/redis';

export const FUNNEL = ['page_view', 'hero_cta', 'calc_select', 'calc_compare', 'result_view', 'form_open', 'form_start', 'help_on', 'form_submit', 'lead_ok', 'lead_error'];
export const OTHER_EVENTS = ['consent_accept', 'consent_reject'];
export const EVENTS = [...FUNNEL, ...OTHER_EVENTS];

const day = (d = new Date()) => d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

function redisStore(redis) {
  return {
    kind: 'redis',
    async incrEvent(event, { variant, device, utmContent }) {
      const d = day();
      const p = redis.pipeline();
      p.hincrby(`ev:${d}`, `${variant}:${event}`, 1);
      p.hincrby(`evd:${d}`, `${device}:${event}`, 1);
      p.hincrby(`evc:${d}`, `${utmContent || '-'}:${event}`, 1);
      p.sadd('days', d);
      await p.exec();
    },
    async addLead(lead) { await redis.lpush('leads', JSON.stringify(lead)); },
    async leadCount() { return redis.llen('leads'); },
    async rateLimit(key, limit, windowSec) {
      const k = `rl:${key}`;
      const n = await redis.incr(k);
      if (n === 1) await redis.expire(k, windowSec);
      return n <= limit;
    },
    async raw() {
      const days = (await redis.smembers('days')).sort();
      const out = {};
      for (const d of days) {
        const [ev, evd, evc] = await Promise.all([redis.hgetall(`ev:${d}`), redis.hgetall(`evd:${d}`), redis.hgetall(`evc:${d}`)]);
        out[d] = { ev: ev || {}, evd: evd || {}, evc: evc || {} };
      }
      return out;
    },
  };
}

const mem = { counters: {}, leads: [], rl: new Map() };
function memoryStore() {
  const inc = (d, table, field) => {
    mem.counters[d] ??= { ev: {}, evd: {}, evc: {} };
    mem.counters[d][table][field] = (mem.counters[d][table][field] || 0) + 1;
  };
  return {
    kind: 'memory',
    async incrEvent(event, { variant, device, utmContent }) {
      const d = day();
      inc(d, 'ev', `${variant}:${event}`); inc(d, 'evd', `${device}:${event}`); inc(d, 'evc', `${utmContent || '-'}:${event}`);
    },
    async addLead(lead) { mem.leads.unshift(lead); },
    async leadCount() { return mem.leads.length; },
    async rateLimit(key, limit, windowSec) {
      const now = Date.now(), e = mem.rl.get(key);
      if (!e || now > e.until) { mem.rl.set(key, { n: 1, until: now + windowSec * 1000 }); return true; }
      e.n += 1; return e.n <= limit;
    },
    async raw() { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(mem.counters)); },
    _reset() { mem.counters = {}; mem.leads = []; mem.rl.clear(); },
    _leads() { return mem.leads; },
  };
}

export function getStore() {
  const url = process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL || process.env.KV_REST_API_URL;
  const token = process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_TOKEN || process.env.KV_REST_API_TOKEN;
  return url && token ? redisStore(new Redis({ url, token })) : memoryStore();
}
```

**→ Write** `C:\Users\matus\Desktop\B_project\lib\mailer.js`

```
// Transactional e-mail via Brevo (https://developers.brevo.com/reference/sendtransacemail).
// Without BREVO_API_KEY the mail is kept in an in-memory outbox (tests) and, locally, written to mail-outbox/ for preview.
import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';

const outbox = [];
export const getOutbox = () => outbox;
export const clearOutbox = () => { outbox.length = 0; };

export async function sendMail({ to, toName, subject, html, text, replyTo, tags = [] }) {
  const key = process.env.BREVO_API_KEY;
  if (!key) {
    outbox.push({ to, toName, subject, html, text, replyTo, tags });
    if (!process.env.VERCEL && process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'test') {
      try { mkdirSync('mail-outbox', { recursive: true }); writeFileSync(`mail-outbox/${Date.now()}-${tags[0] || 'mail'}.html`, html); } catch { /* preview only */ }
      console.log(`[mail:console] → ${to} | ${subject}`);
    }
    return { mock: true };
  }
  const from = process.env.MAIL_FROM;
  if (!from) throw new Error('MAIL_FROM is not set');
  const res = await fetch('https://api.brevo.com/v3/smtp/email', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'api-key': key, 'content-type': 'application/json', accept: 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      sender: { email: from, name: process.env.MAIL_FROM_NAME || 'Veenos' },
      to: [{ email: to, ...(toName ? { name: toName } : {}) }],
      subject, htmlContent: html, textContent: text,
      ...(replyTo ? { replyTo } : {}),
      ...(tags.length ? { tags } : {}),
    }),
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Brevo ${res.status}: ${(await res.text()).slice(0, 200)}`);
  return res.json();
}
```

**→ Write** `C:\Users\matus\Desktop\B_project\lib\validate.js`

```
// Server-side validation of the lead form. Client values are never trusted: numbers are recomputed from monthly/years.
import { isAllowed } from '../public/js/model.js';

export const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]{2,}$/;
export const PHONE_RE = /^\+?[0-9 ]{9,16}$/;
const DEVICES = ['mobile', 'tablet', 'desktop'];
const clean = (s, max) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f<>]/g, '').trim().slice(0, max);
export const MIN_FILL_MS = 2000;

export function validateLead(b = {}) {
  // bots: honeypot filled or form submitted faster than a human could → pretend success, do nothing
  if (clean(b.company, 100) || !(Number(b.elapsedMs) >= MIN_FILL_MS)) return { ok: false, spam: true };
  const name = clean(b.name, 80);
  const email = clean(b.email, 120).toLowerCase();
  const help = b.help === true;
  const phone = help ? clean(b.phone, 20).replace(/\s+/g, ' ') : '';
  const monthly = Number(b.monthly), years = Number(b.years);
  if (!name) return { ok: false, error: 'name' };
  if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email)) return { ok: false, error: 'email' };
  if (help && !PHONE_RE.test(phone)) return { ok: false, error: 'phone' };
  if (!isAllowed(monthly, years)) return { ok: false, error: 'input' };
  return {
    ok: true,
    lead: {
      name, email, help, phone, tips: b.tips === true, monthly, years,
      variant: b.variant === 'b' ? 'b' : 'a',
      device: DEVICES.includes(b.device) ? b.device : 'mobile',
      utm_source: clean(b.utm_source, 60), utm_medium: clean(b.utm_medium, 60),
      utm_campaign: clean(b.utm_campaign, 60), utm_content: clean(b.utm_content, 60),
    },
  };
}

const site = () => (process.env.SITE_URL || 'https://veenos.vercel.app').replace(/\/$/, '');

```
// POST /api/lead: validate, store the lead, send the comparison e-mail (and a call-back notice to the team).
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { getStore } from '../lib/store.js';
import { sendMail } from '../lib/mailer.js';
import { validateLead } from '../lib/validate.js';
import { renderComparisonEmail, renderTeamEmail } from '../lib/email-template.js';

export const ipKey = req => {
  const ip = String(req.headers?.['x-forwarded-for'] || '').split(',')[0].trim() || req.socket?.remoteAddress || 'unknown';
  return createHash('sha256').update(ip + new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)).digest('hex').slice(0, 16); // no raw IP stored
};

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return res.status(405).json({ ok: false, error: 'method' });
  let body = req.body;
  if (typeof body === 'string') { try { body = JSON.parse(body); } catch { body = {}; } }

  const v = validateLead(body || {});
  if (!v.ok) return v.spam ? res.status(200).json({ ok: true }) : res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: v.error });

  const store = getStore();
  if (!(await store.rateLimit('lead:' + ipKey(req), 5, 3600))) return res.status(429).json({ ok: false, error: 'rate' });

  const lead = { ...v.lead, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() };
  const meta = { variant: lead.variant, device: lead.device, utmContent: lead.utm_content };
  await store.addLead(lead);

  try {
    const mail = renderComparisonEmail(lead);
    await sendMail({ to: lead.email, toName: lead.name, subject: mail.subject, html: mail.html, text: mail.text, tags: ['srovnani'] });
    if (lead.help && process.env.TEAM_EMAIL) {
      const t = renderTeamEmail(lead);
      await sendMail({ to: process.env.TEAM_EMAIL, subject: t.subject, html: t.html, text: t.text, replyTo: { email: lead.email, name: lead.name }, tags: ['callback'] });
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[lead] mail failed:', e.message);
    await store.incrEvent('lead_error', meta);
    return res.status(502).json({ ok: false, error: 'mail' });
  }

  await store.incrEvent('lead_ok', meta);
  return res.status(200).json({ ok: true });
}
```

```
// POST /api/event: cookie-free funnel beacon (navigator.sendBeacon). Only whitelisted events are counted;
// no IP, no identifiers are stored, only daily counters per variant, device and utm_content.
import { getStore, EVENTS } from '../lib/store.js';
import { ipKey } from './lead.js';

const SERVER_ONLY = ['lead_ok', 'lead_error']; // set by /api/lead, not by the browser

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return res.status(405).end();
  let b = req.body;
  if (typeof b === 'string') { try { b = JSON.parse(b); } catch { b = {}; } }
  b = b || {};
  if (!EVENTS.includes(b.e) || SERVER_ONLY.includes(b.e)) return res.status(204).end();
  const store = getStore();
  if (!(await store.rateLimit('ev:' + ipKey(req), 300, 3600))) return res.status(204).end();
  await store.incrEvent(b.e, {
    variant: b.v === 'b' ? 'b' : 'a',
    device: ['mobile', 'tablet', 'desktop'].includes(b.d) ? b.d : 'mobile',
    utmContent: String(b.utm_content || '').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_\-.]/g, '').slice(0, 60),
  });
  return res.status(204).end();
}
```

```
// GET /api/stats?token=…  (add &format=html for a readable table)
// Funnel from arrival to submitted form, per A/B variant, device and ad (utm_content). Protected by STATS_TOKEN in production.
import { getStore, FUNNEL, OTHER_EVENTS } from '../lib/store.js';

const LABELS = {
  page_view: 'Příchod', hero_cta: 'Klik na CTA v hero', calc_select: 'Výběr v kalkulačce', calc_compare: 'Porovnat 4 fondy',
  result_view: 'Výsledek viděn', form_open: 'Otevřel formulář', form_start: 'Začal vyplňovat', help_on: '„Chci pomoct" zapnuto',
  form_submit: 'Odeslal formulář', lead_ok: 'Lead uložen + e-mail odeslán', lead_error: 'Chyba odeslání',
  consent_accept: 'Cookies: přijal', consent_reject: 'Cookies: odmítl',
};

export function aggregate(raw) {
  const out = { byVariant: {}, byDevice: {}, byAd: {}, byDay: {}, total: {} };
  const add = (bucket, key, event, n) => { bucket[key] ??= {}; bucket[key][event] = (bucket[key][event] || 0) + Number(n); };
  for (const [day, t] of Object.entries(raw)) {
    for (const [field, n] of Object.entries(t.ev || {})) { const [v, e] = field.split(':'); add(out.byVariant, v, e, n); add(out.byDay, day, e, n); out.total[e] = (out.total[e] || 0) + Number(n); }
    for (const [field, n] of Object.entries(t.evd || {})) { const [d, e] = field.split(':'); add(out.byDevice, d, e, n); }
    for (const [field, n] of Object.entries(t.evc || {})) { const i = field.lastIndexOf(':'); add(out.byAd, field.slice(0, i), field.slice(i + 1), n); }
  }
  out.conversion = {};
  for (const [v, c] of Object.entries(out.byVariant)) out.conversion[v] = c.page_view ? +(100 * (c.lead_ok || 0) / c.page_view).toFixed(2) : null;
  return out;
}

const pct = (a, b) => (b ? (100 * a / b).toFixed(1) + ' %' : '–');
function table(title, groups) {
  const keys = Object.keys(groups).sort();
  if (!keys.length) return `<h2>${title}</h2><p>Zatím žádná data.</p>`;
  const head = `<tr><th>Krok</th>${keys.map(k => `<th>${k}</th><th>% z příchodů</th>`).join('')}</tr>`;
  const rows = [...FUNNEL, ...OTHER_EVENTS].map(e => `<tr><td>${LABELS[e]}</td>${keys.map(k => { const n = groups[k][e] || 0; return `<td>${n}</td><td>${pct(n, groups[k].page_view || 0)}</td>`; }).join('')}</tr>`).join('');
  return `<h2>${title}</h2><table>${head}${rows}</table>`;
}

export default async function handler(req, res) {
  const token = process.env.STATS_TOKEN;
  const isProd = !!process.env.VERCEL;
  if ((isProd && !token) || (token && req.query?.token !== token)) return res.status(403).json({ ok: false, error: 'forbidden' });
  const store = getStore();
  const data = aggregate(await store.raw());
  data.leads = await store.leadCount();
  data.store = store.kind;
  if (req.query?.format !== 'html') return res.status(200).json(data);
  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/html; charset=utf-8');
  res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-store');
  return res.status(200).send(`<!doctype html><html lang="cs"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>Veenos: funnel</title>
<style>body{font:14px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;background:#18191c;color:#f3f3ef;padding:24px;max-width:1100px;margin:auto}h1{font-size:24px}h2{font-size:17px;margin-top:28px}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin-top:8px}th,td{border-bottom:1px solid #2c2d32;padding:7px 10px;text-align:right}th:first-child,td:first-child{text-align:left}th{color:#a3a49f;font-weight:600}.k{color:#cfe68a;font-weight:800}</style></head><body>
<h1>Veenos: funnel od příchodu po odeslaný formulář</h1>
<p>Leadů celkem: <span class="k">${data.leads}</span> · konverze (lead / příchod): A ${data.conversion.a ?? '–'} %, B ${data.conversion.b ?? '–'} % · úložiště: ${data.store}</p>
${table('Podle varianty (A/B)', data.byVariant)}${table('Podle zařízení', data.byDevice)}${table('Podle reklamy (utm_content)', data.byAd)}
</body></html>`);
}
```

import test, { beforeEach } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';

process.env.NODE_ENV = 'test';
delete process.env.BREVO_API_KEY;
delete process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL;
delete process.env.KV_REST_API_URL;

const { default: lead } = await import('../api/lead.js');
const { default: event } = await import('../api/event.js');
const { default: stats } = await import('../api/stats.js');
const { getOutbox, clearOutbox } = await import('../lib/mailer.js');
const { getStore } = await import('../lib/store.js');

let ipCounter = 0;
function call(handler, { method = 'POST', body = {}, query = {}, ip } = {}) {
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    const res = { statusCode: 200, headers: {}, body: undefined };
    res.status = c => { res.statusCode = c; return res; };
    res.json = o => { res.body = o; resolve(res); return res; };
    res.send = t => { res.body = t; resolve(res); return res; };
    res.end = () => { resolve(res); return res; };
    res.setHeader = (k, v) => { res.headers[k] = v; };
    handler({ method, body, query, headers: { 'x-forwarded-for': ip || `10.0.0.${++ipCounter}` } }, res);
  });
}
const valid = (extra = {}) => ({ name: 'Jan Novák', email: 'jan@example.cz', monthly: 3000, years: 20, help: false, tips: false, company: '', elapsedMs: 8000, variant: 'a', device: 'mobile', utm_content: 'ad_a', ...extra });

beforeEach(() => { clearOutbox(); getStore()._reset(); delete process.env.TEAM_EMAIL; });

test('valid lead → 200, stored, one comparison e-mail to the visitor', async () => {
  const r = await call(lead, { body: valid() });
  assert.equal(r.statusCode, 200); assert.equal(r.body.ok, true);
  assert.equal(getStore()._leads().length, 1);
  assert.equal(getOutbox().length, 1);
  assert.equal(getOutbox()[0].to, 'jan@example.cz');
  assert.match(getOutbox()[0].subject, /CSPX vychází nejlevněji/);
});

test('"Chci pomoct" with phone → second e-mail to the team', async () => {
  process.env.TEAM_EMAIL = 'team@example.cz';
  const r = await call(lead, { body: valid({ help: true, phone: '+420 777 123 456' }) });
  assert.equal(r.statusCode, 200);
  assert.equal(getOutbox().length, 2);
  assert.equal(getOutbox()[1].to, 'team@example.cz');
  assert.match(getOutbox()[0].html, /Brzy vám zavoláme/);
});

test('validation errors → 400', async () => {
  assert.equal((await call(lead, { body: valid({ email: 'nope' }) })).body.error, 'email');
  assert.equal((await call(lead, { body: valid({ name: '  ' }) })).body.error, 'name');
  assert.equal((await call(lead, { body: valid({ help: true, phone: '' }) })).body.error, 'phone');
  assert.equal((await call(lead, { body: valid({ monthly: 2500 }) })).body.error, 'input');
  assert.equal(getOutbox().length, 0);
});

test('phone is ignored when help is off', async () => {
  await call(lead, { body: valid({ help: false, phone: '+420 777 123 456' }) });
  assert.equal(getStore()._leads()[0].phone, '');
});

test('bots (honeypot / too fast) get 200 but nothing is stored or sent', async () => {
  await call(lead, { body: valid({ company: 'Spam s.r.o.' }) });
  await call(lead, { body: valid({ elapsedMs: 300 }) });
  assert.equal(getStore()._leads().length, 0);
  assert.equal(getOutbox().length, 0);
});

test('rate limit: 6th lead from the same IP within an hour → 429', async () => {
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) assert.equal((await call(lead, { body: valid(), ip: '1.2.3.4' })).statusCode, 200);
  assert.equal((await call(lead, { body: valid(), ip: '1.2.3.4' })).statusCode, 429);
});

test('events: whitelisted only, counted per variant; stats aggregate the funnel', async () => {
  await call(event, { body: JSON.stringify({ e: 'page_view', v: 'b', d: 'tablet', utm_content: 'ad_b' }) });

  await call(event, { body: JSON.stringify({ e: 'evil', v: 'b' }) });         // unknown

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { renderComparisonEmail, renderTeamEmail } from '../lib/email-template.js';
import { compare, formatCzk } from '../public/js/model.js';

test('e-mail contains all four funds with the model numbers', () => {
  const { html, text, subject } = renderComparisonEmail({ monthly: 3000, years: 20 });
  for (const r of compare(3000, 20)) {
    assert.ok(html.includes(`>${r.ticker}<`) || html.includes(`<b>${r.ticker}</b>`), r.ticker);
    assert.ok(html.includes(formatCzk(r.total)), `${r.ticker} total`);
    assert.ok(text.includes(`${r.ticker}: ${formatCzk(r.total)}`));
  }
  assert.equal(subject, 'Vaše srovnání S&P 500 ETF: CSPX vychází nejlevněji');
  assert.match(html, /nejde o investiční doporučení/);
});

test('call-back block only when help is on; phone is masked', () => {
  assert.doesNotMatch(renderComparisonEmail({ monthly: 3000, years: 20 }).html, /Brzy vám zavoláme/);
  const { html } = renderComparisonEmail({ monthly: 3000, years: 20, help: true, phone: '+420 777 123 456' });
  assert.match(html, /Brzy vám zavoláme/);
  assert.ok(html.includes('456'));
  assert.ok(!html.includes('777 123'));
});

test('team notice escapes HTML in user input', () => {
  const t = renderTeamEmail({ name: '<script>x</script>', email: 'a@b.cz', phone: '+420777123456', monthly: 3000, years: 20, variant: 'a', device: 'mobile', utm_content: '' });
  assert.ok(!t.html.includes('<script>'));
  assert.ok(t.html.includes('&lt;script&gt;'));
});
```

  <h1>Ochrana osobních údajů</h1>
  <p class="meta">Platné od 11. 10. 2026</p>

  <h2>Kdo údaje zpracovává</h2>
  <p>Správce: <b>[název provozovatele, IČO, sídlo]</b>, kontakt: <b>[e-mail]</b>.</p>

  <h2>Jaké údaje a proč</h2>
  <table>
    <tr><th>Údaje</th><th>Účel</th><th>Právní základ</th><th>Jak dlouho</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Jméno, e-mail, zadání kalkulačky (částka, doba)</td><td>Zaslání srovnání, o které jste požádali</td><td>Kroky na vaši žádost (čl. 6 odst. 1 písm. b GDPR)</td><td>12 měsíců</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Telefon (jen když zapnete „Chci pomoct")</td><td>Jeden telefonát k vašemu výpočtu a dalšímu postupu</td><td>Váš souhlas (čl. 6 odst. 1 písm. a GDPR)</td><td>Do uskutečnění hovoru, nejdéle 3 měsíce</td></tr>
    <tr><td>E-mail (jen když zaškrtnete „Občas mi pošlete tipy")</td><td>Občasné tipy e-mailem</td><td>Souhlas (čl. 6 odst. 1 písm. a GDPR, zákon č. 480/2004 Sb.)</td><td>Do odhlášení</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Souhrnné počty kroků na stránce (bez cookies, bez IP adresy, bez identifikátorů)</td><td>Měření, kolik lidí projde jednotlivými kroky</td><td>Oprávněný zájem (čl. 6 odst. 1 písm. f GDPR)</td><td>Souhrnná čísla</td></tr>
  </table>
  <p>Volitelně ukládáme i variantu stránky a zdroj reklamy (parametry utm), abychom věděli, co funguje. Telefonát slouží k pomoci s postupem. Nejde o investiční poradenství.</p>

  <h2>Kdo údaje zpracovává za nás</h2>
  <ul>
    <li><b>Vercel</b>: hosting webu</li>
    <li><b>Upstash</b>: databáze (region EU)</li>
    <li><b>Brevo</b>: odesílání e-mailů</li>

  </ul>

  <h2>Vaše práva</h2>

Stížnost můžete podat u Úřadu pro ochranu osobních údajů (<a href="https://uoou.gov.cz" target="_blank" rel="noopener">uoou.gov.cz</a>).</p>

<!doctype html>
<html lang="cs">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Návod: jak koupit ETF u brokera | Veenos</title>
<meta name="description" content="Obecný postup krok za krokem: výběr brokera, poplatky, ISIN, měna, pokyn a daně.">
<meta name="theme-color" content="#18191c">
<link rel="icon" href="/favicon.svg" type="image/svg+xml">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="/fonts/manrope.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="/css/page.css">
</head>
<body>
<header class="top"><div class="wrap"><a class="logo" href="/"><svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2.5 8.5 L9.5 19 L20.5 4.5" stroke="#cfe68a" stroke-width="3.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path d="M14.5 4 H21 V10.5" stroke="#cfe68a" stroke-width="3.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>veenos</a><a class="back" href="/">← Zpět na srovnání</a></div></header>
<main class="wrap doc">
  <h1>Jak koupit ETF u brokera</h1>
  <p class="lead">Obecný postup pro nákup fondu z Česka. Nejde o doporučení konkrétního fondu ani brokera. Co je pro vás vhodné, záleží na vaší situaci.</p>

  <ol class="steps">
    <li><h2>Vyberte si regulovaného brokera</h2><p>Ověřte, že broker má licenci od ČNB nebo jiného regulátora v EU. Seznam subjektů pod dohledem najdete na <a href="https://www.cnb.cz/cs/dohled-financni-trh/seznamy/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">webu ČNB</a>.</p></li>
    <li><h2>Porovnejte celkové náklady</h2><p>Nejen poplatek fondu, ale i provizi za nákup, směnu korun na dolary nebo eura a případný poplatek za vedení účtu. Právě směna a provize často rozhodují víc než poplatek fondu. <a href="/#calc">Spočítat v korunách</a></p></li>
    <li><h2>Najděte fond podle ISIN</h2><p>Stejný fond se obchoduje pod různými zkratkami na různých burzách. Jednoznačný je kód ISIN. Evropské (UCITS) fondy mají ISIN obvykle začínající <b>IE</b> nebo <b>LU</b>; například iShares Core S&amp;P 500 UCITS má ISIN IE00B5BMR087.</p></li>
    <li><h2>Zvolte burzu a měnu</h2><p>Některé fondy se obchodují v USD (Londýn), jiné v EUR (Xetra). Pokud investujete z korun, každá směna něco stojí. Zjistěte si, jaký kurz broker používá.</p></li>
    <li><h2>Zadejte pokyn</h2><p>Limitní pokyn určuje nejvyšší cenu, za kterou nakoupíte. U pravidelného investování si nastavte stejnou částku každý měsíc nebo čtvrtletí.</p></li>
    <li><h2>Myslete na daně</h2><p>V Česku je prodej cenných papírů osvobozen od daně po 3 letech držení (časový test) nebo když celkové příjmy z prodeje za rok nepřesáhnou 100 000 Kč (hodnotový test). Limit 40 milionů Kč u časového testu byl pro cenné papíry od roku 2026 zrušen. U dividend z amerických fondů se obvykle sráží 15 % daně (s formulářem W-8BEN). Konkrétní daňové dopady závisí na vaší situaci (<a href="https://financnisprava.gov.cz/cs/financni-sprava/media-a-verejnost/tiskove-zpravy-gfr/tiskove-zpravy-2026/danove-novinky-pro-rok-2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Finanční správa</a>).</p></li>
  </ol>

  <p class="risk">Investování nese riziko ztráty, hodnota investice může klesat i stoupat a minulé výnosy nejsou zárukou budoucích. Veenos není investiční poradce ani obchodník s cennými papíry.</p>
</main>
<footer class="foot"><div class="wrap">© 2026 Veenos · Ukázkový projekt · <a href="/">Srovnání</a> · <a href="/ochrana-udaju">Ochrana údajů</a></div></footer>
</body>
</html>
```

/* Shared style for the text subpages (návod, ochrana údajů). The landing page keeps its CSS inline for speed. */
:root { --bg:#18191c; --bg2:#141518; --fg:#f3f3ef; --muted:#a3a49f; --line:#2c2d32; --card:#202125; --accent:#cfe68a; }
* { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
body { background: var(--bg); color: var(--fg); font: 16px/1.6 "Manrope", system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif; -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased; }
a { color: var(--accent); text-underline-offset: 3px; }
:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--accent); outline-offset: 3px; }
.wrap { max-width: 760px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 0 22px; }
.top { border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.top .wrap { height: 64px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; }
.logo { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-weight: 800; font-size: 21px; letter-spacing: -0.045em; color: var(--fg); text-decoration: none; }
.back { font-size: 14px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--muted); text-decoration: none; }
.doc { padding: 40px 22px 64px; }
.doc h1 { font-size: clamp(32px, 8vw, 46px); line-height: 1.1; letter-spacing: -0.045em; font-weight: 800; margin-bottom: 12px; }
.doc h2 { font-size: 20px; letter-spacing: -0.02em; font-weight: 800; margin: 32px 0 8px; }
.doc p, .doc li { color: #d4d5d0; }
.doc p + p { margin-top: 10px; }
.doc .lead { font-size: 18px; color: var(--muted); }
.doc .meta { color: var(--muted); font-size: 14px; }
.doc ul { padding-left: 20px; display: grid; gap: 6px; }
.doc table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin: 10px 0; font-size: 14px; }
.doc th, .doc td { text-align: left; vertical-align: top; padding: 10px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.doc th { color: var(--muted); font-weight: 700; font-size: 12.5px; }
.doc code { background: var(--card); padding: 1px 6px; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 13px; }
.note { background: rgba(207,230,138,.1); border: 1px solid rgba(207,230,138,.35); border-radius: 14px; padding: 12px 16px; font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 24px; }
.steps { list-style: none; counter-reset: s; margin-top: 18px; display: grid; gap: 12px; }
.steps li { counter-increment: s; background: var(--card); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 20px; padding: 20px 20px 18px 64px; position: relative; }
.steps li::before { content: counter(s); position: absolute; left: 20px; top: 20px; width: 30px; height: 30px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--accent); color: #18191c; font-weight: 800; display: grid; place-items: center; }
.steps h2 { margin: 2px 0 6px; font-size: 18px; }
.risk { margin-top: 28px; font-size: 13.5px; color: var(--muted); }
.foot { border-top: 1px solid var(--line); background: var(--bg2); padding: 24px 0; font-size: 13px; color: var(--muted); }
@media (max-width: 560px) { .doc table, .doc thead, .doc tbody, .doc tr, .doc td, .doc th { display: block; } .doc tr { border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); padding: 8px 0; } .doc th { display: none; } .doc td { border: 0; padding: 2px 0; } .doc td:first-child { font-weight: 700; color: var(--fg); } }
```

@import url("../public/fonts/manrope.css");
:root { --bg:#18191c; --fg:#f3f3ef; --muted:#a3a49f; --line:#2c2d32; --card:#202125; --accent:#cfe68a; }
* { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
html, body { width: 100%; height: 100%; }
body { background: var(--bg); color: var(--fg); font-family: "Manrope", sans-serif; -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased; overflow: hidden; }
.tab { font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.frame { position: relative; width: 100%; height: 100%; display: flex; flex-direction: column; padding: 84px 84px 72px; overflow: hidden; }
.logo { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 16px; font-weight: 800; font-size: 46px; letter-spacing: -0.045em; }
.logo svg { width: 46px; height: 46px; }
.h { font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: -0.055em; line-height: 1.04; }
.h em { font-style: normal; color: var(--accent); }
.fine { font-size: 24px; color: #8f908b; line-height: 1.4; }
.cta { align-self: flex-start; background: var(--accent); color: #18191c; font-weight: 800; font-size: 40px; letter-spacing: -0.02em; border-radius: 999px; padding: 26px 46px; }
.bars { display: grid; gap: 34px; }
.br .t { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: baseline; margin-bottom: 14px; }
.br .t b { font-size: 48px; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: -0.03em; }
.br .t span { font-size: 44px; font-weight: 800; }
.br .t small { font-size: 26px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--muted); margin-left: 14px; letter-spacing: 0; }
.br .track { height: 22px; border-radius: 99px; background: #2a2b30; overflow: hidden; }
.br .fill { height: 100%; border-radius: 99px; background: #6e6f6a; }
.br.w .fill { background: var(--accent); } .br.w .t span { color: var(--accent); }
.glow { position: absolute; width: 900px; height: 900px; border-radius: 50%; background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(207,230,138,.16), rgba(207,230,138,0) 65%); right: -320px; top: -260px; pointer-events: none; }
```

**→ Write** `C:\Users\matus\Desktop\B_project\scripts\render-ads.js`

```
// Renders the ad creatives and the share image to PNG with headless Edge/Chrome (no extra dependencies).
//   node scripts/render-ads.js
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { existsSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const BROWSERS = [
  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Microsoft/Edge/Application/msedge.exe',
  'C:/Program Files/Microsoft/Edge/Application/msedge.exe',
  'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
  '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome',
  '/usr/bin/google-chrome', '/usr/bin/chromium',
];
const browser = BROWSERS.find(existsSync);
if (!browser) { console.error('No Edge/Chrome found'); process.exit(1); }

const JOBS = [
  ['ads/ad-a.html', 'f=feed', 1080, 1350, 'ads/png/ad-a-feed-1080x1350.png'],
  ['ads/ad-a.html', 'f=story', 1080, 1920, 'ads/png/ad-a-story-1080x1920.png'],
  ['ads/ad-b.html', 'f=feed', 1080, 1350, 'ads/png/ad-b-feed-1080x1350.png'],
  ['ads/ad-b.html', 'f=story', 1080, 1920, 'ads/png/ad-b-story-1080x1920.png'],
  ['ads/og.html', '', 1200, 630, 'public/og.png'],
];
mkdirSync('ads/png', { recursive: true });
for (const [file, query, w, h, out] of JOBS) {
  const url = pathToFileURL(resolve(file)).href + (query ? '?' + query : '');
  execFileSync(browser, ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--hide-scrollbars', '--force-device-scale-factor=1',
    `--window-size=${w},${h}`, '--virtual-time-budget=3000', `--screenshot=${resolve(out)}`, url], { stdio: 'ignore' });
  console.log('rendered', out);
}
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-10-11 07:38:43

The images are written a moment after Edge's launcher process exits. I'll make the script wait for each file, then look at the creatives.

**→ Bash** — Make the render script wait for output and re-render

```
rm -f ads/png/test.png && python -I - scripts/render-ads.js <<'PYEOF'
import sys
p=sys.argv[1]; s=open(p,encoding='utf-8').read()
s=s.replace("import { existsSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';","import { existsSync, mkdirSync, rmSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';")
s=s.replace("""mkdirSync('ads/png', { recursive: true });""","""mkdirSync('ads/png', { recursive: true });
const sleep = ms => Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, ms);
// Edge's launcher returns before the screenshot is written: wait until the file exists and its size is stable.
function waitFor(file, timeoutMs = 20000) {
  const start = Date.now(); let last = -1;
  while (Date.now() - start < timeoutMs) {
    if (existsSync(file)) { const size = statSync(file).size; if (size > 0 && size === last) return true; last = size; }
    sleep(300);
  }
  return false;
}""")

# Reklamy: dve verzie (Meta: Facebook + Instagram)

Obe verzie používajú rovnaký uhol **„stejný index, levnější dvojče"** (rozhodnutie D8/D8b v `research/decisions.md`) v dvoch rôznych exekúciách. Prvá obrazovka stránky nadväzuje na obe („Stejný index. Jiná cena."), takže sa zachová message match (+39–48 % v dvoch zdokumentovaných testoch, `research/99-verification-log.md` C10).

Prečo len Meta: Google Ads vyžaduje od septembra 2026 v ČR overenie finančného inzerenta, ktoré nelicencovaný vydavateľ nedostane. TikTok povoľuje investičné služby len licencovaným subjektom (`research/04-ads-compliance.md`, overené D1, D2). Pri Mete zostáva otvorená otázka, či overenie vyžaduje aj pre vzdelávací porovnávač (D6).

| | Verzia A: srovnávací karta | Verzia B: otázka |
|---|---|---|
| Kreatíva feed (1080×1350) | `png/ad-a-feed-1080x1350.png` | `png/ad-b-feed-1080x1350.png` |
| Kreatíva stories/reels (1080×1920) | `png/ad-a-story-1080x1920.png` | `png/ad-b-story-1080x1920.png` |
| Primárny text | VOO, IVV, SPY i evropský CSPX kopírují stejný index S&P 500. Z Česka je ale nakoupíte za úplně jiné peníze. Spočítejte si, kolik vás vaše cesta stojí v korunách. | Kupujete S&P 500? Fond a broker rozhodují o tom, kolik vám z výnosu zůstane. Porovnejte 4 fondy na stejný index v korunách. |
| Nadpis | Stejný index. Jiná cena. | Kolik vás stojí vaše S&P 500? |
| Popis | Srovnání S&P 500 ETF v korunách | 4 fondy, 2 odpovědi, výsledek hned |
| Tlačidlo (Meta CTA) | Další informace | Další informace |
| Cieľová URL | `https://<doména>/?utm_source=meta&utm_medium=paid_social&utm_campaign=sp500_srovnani&utm_content=ad_a` | `…&utm_content=ad_b` |

Poznámky ku compliance (ESMA/ČNB, `research/04-ads-compliance.md`):
- Žiadne „bezpečné", „garantované" ani „nejlepší"; čísla sú vždy s predpokladom (3 000 Kč mesačne, 20 rokov) a poznámkou „ilustrativní výpočet".
- Na kreatíve je upozornenie na riziko a „nejde o investiční doporučení". Disclaimer sám osebe nechráni (ESMA 2026), preto obsah zostáva neosobný a informačný.
- Cielenie: ČR, 18–65+, bez cielenia na pohlavie (ako konkurencia v Meta Ad Library). Segment z D1 (DIY investor, ktorý pozná S&P 500) sa rieši textom, nie cielením.

Meranie: `utm_content` (ad_a / ad_b) sa ukladá ku každej udalosti funnelu aj k leadu. `/api/stats?format=html` ukazuje funnel podľa reklamy aj podľa varianty stránky.

Nové vyrenderovanie: `node scripts/render-ads.js` (headless Edge/Chrome).
```

# Funnel-based conversion estimate + A/B sample size (for README)
low, mid, high = (0.30, 0.25, 0.45), (0.42, 0.35, 0.55), (0.50, 0.40, 0.60)
for name, (a, b, c) in [('low', low), ('mid', mid), ('high', high)]:
    print(name, f"{a*b*c*100:.1f}%")
p, rel = 0.06, 0.20
d = p * rel
n = 16 * p * (1 - p) / d**2

p2, d2 = 0.55, 0.55 * 0.10
print('n per arm form completion (55%, +10% rel):', round(16 * p2 * (1 - p2) / d2**2))
p3, d3 = 0.42, 0.42 * 0.15
print('n per arm calc start (42%, +15% rel):', round(16 * p3 * (1 - p3) / d3**2))
# CPL rough: CPC 8-12 CZK
for cpc in (8, 12):
    print('CPL at', cpc, 'Kč/klik:', round(cpc / 0.06), '(6%)', round(cpc / 0.04), '(4%)', round(cpc / 0.08), '(8%)')
EOF

```
# Veenos: porovnanie S&P 500 ETF z NYSE pre českého investora (Zadanie B)

**Stránka:** `<URL po nasadení>` · **Repo:** https://github.com/Matush77/Clientelo-task-b
**Reklamy:** [`ads/`](ads/) · **Meranie funnelu:** `/api/stats?token=…&format=html` · **Export konverzácií:** [`ai-log/`](ai-log/)

Mobilná lead-magnet stránka v češtine. Návštevník z reklamy za pár sekúnd vidí, že **SPY, IVV a VOO (NYSE Arca) a európsky CSPX sledujú rovnaký index S&P 500, ale z Česka ho stoja úplne iné peniaze**. Dvoma ťuknutiami (suma, roky) dostane porovnanie štyroch fondov v korunách. Za meno a e-mail mu príde podrobný rozpis; telefón dá len vtedy, keď sám chce pomôcť („Chci pomoct").

> Projekt je ukážka pre výberové konanie. Všetko, čo by pri ostrom spustení ešte chýbalo, je v časti [Čo chýba](#čo-chýba).

---

## 1. Cieľová skupina
**„Kutil", ktorý pozná S&P 500:** investuje sám (XTB, Trading 212, Degiro) alebo s tým práve začína, tickery pozná z YouTube a sociálnych sietí a rieši poplatky, menu a dane. Prečo práve on:
- Na českom fóre FinExpert sa najviac diskutuje o UCITS tickeroch (VWCE 185 príspevkov, IWDA 141, SPYL 106, SXR8 78), o TER, FX a časovom teste (`research/01-audience.md`, citácie overené v logu A6).
- 39 % Čechov investuje; neinvestorov brzdí strach zo straty (73 %), nedostatok peňazí (71 %) a znalostí (71 %); hlavná motivácia je dôchodok (78 %). Raiffeisenbank, 7/2026, overené A2.
- Meta cielenie je aj tak široké (konkurencia beží na celú ČR, 18–65+). Segment preto určuje **text a hĺbku obsahu, nie cielenie**.

## 2. Čo návštevník dostane a kedy žiadame kontakt
| Kedy | Čo dostane | Čo dá |
|---|---|---|
| Hneď po 2 ťuknutiach | 4 fondy zoradené podľa celkových nákladov v Kč (poplatok fondu + nákup z ČR) | nič |
| Po kliknutí „Poslat srovnání e-mailem" | e-mail: celý rozpis po fondoch, čo z toho plynie, predpoklady a zdroje, návod na nákup ETF | meno + e-mail (autofill) |
| Voliteľne „Chci pomoct" | jeden telefonát k postupu (broker, výpočet) | telefón |

**Hodnota je viditeľná ešte pred formulárom** (požiadavka zadania), formulár sa otvorí až po výsledku. Rovnaký vzor používajú Portu, Fondee aj Finax (`research/02-competitors.md`). Telefón je opt-in: kontakt je kvalitnejší a zároveň máme výslovný súhlas s hovorom (§96 zák. 127/2005 Sb., `research/decisions.md` D5b, D5c).

## 3. Poradie sekcií a prečo
1. **Hero:** „Stejný index. Jiná cena." + 1 veta + 3 krátke odrážky + 1 CTA. Nadväzuje na obe reklamy (message match +39–48 % v dvoch testoch, log C10) a prejde 2-sekundovým testom: čo to je, čo z toho mám, kam kliknúť. Na desktope je kalkulačka priamo v hero vedľa nadpisu.
2. **Kalkulačka:** len 2 voľby (mesačne, roky) na jednej karte, žiadne písanie. Podľa zadania sú interaktívne prvky cestou ku kontaktu.
3. **Výsledok:** 4 pruhy na jednej škále, najlacnejší zvýraznený. Toto je samotné **porovnanie ETF z NYSE**, ktoré zadanie žiada, s európskym dvojčaťom ako kontrolou z pohľadu Čecha.
4. **Formulár (bottom sheet / dialóg):** až po výsledku, 2 povinné polia, autofill.
5. **Jak počítáme:** zdroje a predpoklady pre skeptikov, pod výsledkom, aby nebrzdili. Počíta sa z rovnakých dát ako kalkulačka.
6. **Pätička:** upozornenie (nejde o investičné odporúčanie), ochrana údajov, nastavenie cookies.

Cookie lišta sa ukáže pri prvej návšteve (mobil: karta, tablet: stred, desktop: lišta cez celú šírku). Kým je viditeľná, hero sa posunie, aby CTA nezakrývala.

## 4. Reklamy (2 verzie)
Detaily, texty a UTM odkazy: [`ads/README.md`](ads/README.md).

| A: porovnávacia karta (VOO = CSPX) | B: otázka „Kupujete S&P 500?" |
|---|---|
| ![A](ads/png/ad-a-feed-1080x1350.png) | ![B](ads/png/ad-b-feed-1080x1350.png) |

Kanál je **Meta (Facebook + Instagram)**: Google Ads vyžaduje od 9/2026 overenie finančného inzerenta a TikTok licenciu (log D1, D2).

## 5. Dáta a zdroje (skutočné, s dátumom)
Jediný zdroj pravdy je [`public/js/model.js`](public/js/model.js). Pri každom údaji je URL a dátum; stránka, e-mail aj testy počítajú z neho.

| Údaj | Hodnota

**Done and tested:**
- **The page** ([public/index.html](public/index.html)) is the v13 design as a working page, tested end to end in the browser on phone, tablet and desktop. The full flow works: cookie banner, calculator, four-fund bars, form with the optional phone, confirmation, and every funnel step counted.
- **The data and calculation** ([public/js/model.js](public/js/model.js)) are the only source of numbers for the page, the e-mail and the tests. Every value carries its source and date.

  - the lead form: checks the input, stores the lead, sends the comparison e-mail, and notifies your team when "Chci pomoct" is on
  - the cookie-free funnel counter
  - the stats overview at `/api/stats?format=html`
- **15 automated tests pass**, covering the numbers, form checks, spam and rate limits, and the e-mail content.
- **Subpages:** a neutral guide to buying an ETF ([public/navod.html](public/navod.html)), linked from the e-mail. A privacy and cookies page ([public/ochrana-udaju.html](public/ochrana-udaju.html)), with the operator details marked as placeholders.
- **Ads:** two versions, each in feed and story format, plus the ad copy and tracking links ([ads/](ads/)). There's also a share image for social media.
- **README in Slovak** ([README.md](README.md)) covers every required section, including how I worked with AI and where it went wrong. Only the live URL is still missing.

**→ Bash** — Decide cookie-banner state before paint and fix contrast

```
python -I - <<'PYEOF'
import re
v=open('public/js/variant.js',encoding='utf-8').read()
v=v.replace("""  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-v', v);
})();""","""  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-v', v);
  // Cookie banner state is also decided before first paint, so the hero doesn't jump (CLS).
  try {
    var c = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('veenos_consent') || 'null');
    var ttl = c && (c.a || c.m) ? 360 * 864e5 : 180 * 864e5;
    if (!c || Date.now() - c.t >= ttl) document.documentElement.classList.add('has-banner');
  } catch (e) { document.documentElement.classList.add('has-banner'); }
})();""")
open('public/js/variant.js','w',encoding='utf-8').write(v)

h=open('public/index.html',encoding='utf-8').read()
n1=h.count('body.has-banner'); n2=h.count('body:not(.has-banner)')
h=h.replace('body.has-banner','html.has-banner').replace('body:not(.has-banner)','html:not(.has-banner)')
h=h.replace('.how .asof { font-size: 12.5px; color: #7f807c; margin-top: 14px; }','.how .asof { font-size: 12.5px; color: #9a9b96; margin-top: 14px; }')
open('public/index.html','w',encoding='utf-8').write(h)

a=open('public/js/app.js',encoding='utf-8').read()
a=a.replace("B.classList.remove('has-banner'); closeDialog('cs-open');","document.documentElement.classList.remove('has-banner'); closeDialog('cs-open');")
a=a.replace("else B.classList.add('has-banner');","else document.documentElement.classList.add('has-banner');")
a=a.replace("const ttl = (c.a || c.m) ? 12 * MONTH : 6 * MONTH;","const ttl = (c.a || c.m) ? 12 * MONTH : 6 * MONTH;  // must match public/js/variant.js")
open('public/js/app.js','w',encoding='utf-8').write(a)
print(n1, n2, 'has-banner refs in app.js:', a.count("B.classList.remove('has-banner')"), a.count("B.classList.add('has-banner')"))
PYEOF
grep -c "9a9b96" public/index.html

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-10-11 12:37:34

Both e-mail problems are fixed and live on production. I haven't sent a new e-mail yet, so neither fix has been confirmed in Gmail.

- **Missing logo:** Gmail removes images embedded directly in the HTML (inline SVG data URIs), so I replaced the logo with a PNG hosted on the site (`veenos.vercel.app/email/logo.png`). It includes the wordmark, so the brand font shows even though e-mail clients don't have Manrope. If images are blocked, the text "veenos" appears instead.
- **Overlapping text:** the four narrow columns in "Z čeho se náklady skládají" are replaced with stacked rows. Each fund now has its ticker and total on one line, then "Poplatek fondu … · Nákup z ČR …", then issuer, exchange and broker. Amounts never wrap mid-number. I checked it in a 390 px browser preview: nothing overlaps.

Your test also confirmed delivery works: Gmail put the e-mail in *Updates*, not spam, and it passed all authentication checks. The README now records this test, the two bugs it caught, and that a local preview alone would have missed them.

Everything is deployed and pushed (`1d61148`).

**Should I send one more test e-mail to your Gmail to confirm the fixes?** It would be the same as before (3 000 Kč · 20 let, "Chci pomoct" off).

After that, all that's left is for you to run `/export` into `ai-log/` and send the repo and site links.
