Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **The assignment** (Slovak hiring task "Zadanie B", deadline **16. 10. 2026**): a mobile lead-magnet landing page comparing NYSE-traded ETFs for a Czech retail investor arriving from an ad, who gives the page a few seconds. Requirements:
     - optimised for contacts
     - must not look like default Claude output
     - define the target group, what the visitor gets and when the contact is asked for, with value visible before the form
     - 2 ad versions; the first screen must continue the ad's promise
     - interactive elements leading to the contact; minimal form fields; think through the post-submit experience
     - real data with sources; measure the whole journey
     - README covering section order rationale, expected conversion, 3 A/B hypotheses ranked by impact, how AI was used (agent prompts, how output was checked, where agents erred), decisions on unclear points, what's missing
     - deploy on free hosting (Vercel)
     - public GitHub repo with commit history
     - `ai-log` folder with `/export` transcripts
   - **Working style:**
     - The user wanted research done with Sonnet 5.5 subagents; the plan shouldn't make design decisions.
     - Design decisions are made together with the user, in many preview iterations.
     - The user asked to "run the export for me automatically".
     - The user approved the build plan ("Write a build plan now. Previews look good and final tweaks can be done after the web is built").
     - When rejecting ExitPlanMode once, the user said the cookie consent must be shown even without metrics, because the project is a showcase.
     - The user approved pushing to GitHub ("I do") and sending the test e-mail to their Gmail.
   - **Latest request:** the e-mail arrived in Gmail (Updates folder), but the logo was missing and the breakdown table texts overlapped. Fix both. This is done and deployed.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Claude Code subagents (Sonnet 5.5, `general-purpose`) with shared rules (VERIFIED/INFERRED tags, sources, options only); lead-agent verification log.
   - Stop/SubagentStop hooks running `python -I scripts/export_ai_log.py` (transcript JSONL → Markdown); untracked `.ai-log-redact`.
   - **Stack:**
     - static site in `public/` plus Vercel Node functions in `api/` (ESM, `"type": "module"`), with `outputDirectory: public`, `cleanUrls`
     - security headers (CSP: self + images.unsplash.com, HSTS)
     - Upstash Redis via the Vercel Marketplace (`KV_REST_API_URL`/`KV_REST_API_TOKEN` or `UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_*`), with an in-memory fallback
     - Brevo transactional API (`https://api.brevo.com/v3/smtp/email`), with a console/outbox fallback
     - self-hosted Manrope via `@fontsource/manrope`
   - **Cost model:**
     - TER SPY 0.0945 %, IVV/VOO 0.03 %, CSPX 0.07 %
     - Fio USD 7.95 per order with ≈3 % FX, bought quarterly; XTB 0 % commission with 0.5 % FX
     - ČNB rate 21.745; 7 % p.a. before costs
     - buy cost depends only on the route; the fee takes the remainder
     - 3 000 Kč over 20 y: CSPX 27 900, IVV/VOO 131 300, SPY 142 100 Kč
   - **A/B:** decided before paint in `variant.js` (`?v=a|b` or random 50/50, nothing stored). A = Prague Castle + "Začít investovat"; B = young man + "Spočítat poplatky".
   - **Cookie-free funnel beacons** (`sendBeacon`, in-memory page ID, no IP): Redis `HINCRBY` per day/variant/device/utm_content; `/api/stats` protected by `STATS_TOKEN`.
   - **Cookie consent showcase:** `localStorage` `veenos_consent` (12 months, or 6 after a refusal); banner state set on `<html>` in the head script (CLS fix); GA4/Pixel loaders inactive with empty IDs in `public/js/config.js`.
   - **Legal context:** PRIIPs/KID blocks US ETFs for EU retail (Fio and Patria are exceptions); ČNB advice = individualised recommendation; §96 ZEK consent for calls; Act 480/2004 marketing consent; ÚOOÚ cookie guidance.
   - **Tooling:**
     - headless Edge rendering for the ads/OG/e-mail logo (async write → wait loop, separate profile)
     - Lighthouse via `npx lighthouse@12`
     - responsive `clamp()` sizing; breakpoints 700 (tablet) and 1100 (desktop)

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`research/`**
     - `assignment.md`
     - `prompts/00-shared-rules.md`, `01-05-*.md` (verbatim agent prompts)
     - reports `01-audience.md` through `05-data-facts.md` (with lead-agent corrections inline)
     - `99-verification-log.md` (35 claims + error patterns)
     - `00-decision-brief.md`
     - `decisions.md` (rounds 1–13, D0–D22)
     - `build-plan.md`
   - **`design/previews/`**
     - `index.html`, `v2`–`v11.html`
     - `v12.html` / `v12-site.html` / `v12-email.html`
     - `v13.html` / `v13-site.html` (approved design)
   - **`scripts/`**
     - `export_ai_log.py`: renders main + subagent transcripts to `ai-log/<session-start-date>_session-<id>.md` and `_agents/`. It redacts `@gmail.com` addresses, `<system-reminder>` blocks, and terms from the untracked `.ai-log-redact` file. File names use `session_start()` (first timestamp).
     - `dev-server.js`: local static server + `/api` router, loads `.env.local`.
     - `copy-fonts.js`: Manrope 400/600/700/800, latin + latin-ext → `public/fonts/manrope.css`.
     - `render-ads.js`: renders the jobs below with headless Edge, using `--user-data-dir=.edge-render-profile` and a `waitFor()` poll. The e-mail logo job is transparent (`--default-background-color=00000000`).
       - ad-a/ad-b feed 1080×1350 and story 1080×1920 → `ads/png/`
       - `og.html` → `public/og.png`
       - `email-logo.html` → `public/email/logo.png` (240×56)
     - `inline_v12_preview.py`: inlines a site and the e-mail into a showcase via srcdoc (args: folder, version).
   - **`public/index.html`:** the production page (v13 design).
     - Head: preloads fonts and runs `<script src="/js/variant.js">`.
     - Sections: hero (photo per `html[data-v]`, `.lab-a`/`.lab-b` CTA labels), calculator (`.js-m`/`.js-y` chips with `aria-pressed`, `.js-go`), `#result` (bars `ol.js-bars`, `.js-open`), `#how` rendered from the model, footer with a `.js-ckfoot` button.
     - Overlays: menu dialog; form `.js-form` (`role=dialog`, name/email, `.js-tg` switch revealing `#f-phone-wrap`, tips checkbox, honeypot `company`, `.js-err`); cookie `.ck` + `.cs` settings.
     - CSS uses `html.has-banner`.
   - **`public/js/variant.js`:**
     ```js
     (function () {
       var q = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('v');
       var v = q === 'a' || q === 'b' ? q : (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'a' : 'b');
       document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-v', v);
       try {
         var c = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('veenos_consent') || 'null');
         var ttl = c && (c.a || c.m) ? 360 * 864e5 : 180 * 864e5;
         if (!c || Date.now() - c.t >= ttl) document.documentElement.classList.add('has-banner');
       } catch (e) { document.documentElement.classList.add('has-banner'); }
     })();
     ```
   - **`public/js/model.js`:** exports `DATA_AS_OF`, `ASSUMPTIONS`, `ROUTES` (fio, xtb with sources/asOf), `FUNDS` (SPY, IVV, VOO, CSPX with source/asOf/issuer/listing), `LISTING_SOURCE`, `ALLOWED` {monthly:[1000,3000,5000,10000], years:[5,10,20,30]}, `futureValue(monthly, years, {ter, fx, commissionCzk, ordersEveryMonths})`, `compare(monthly, years)` (sorted rows {ticker,total,feeCost,buyCost,fund,route}), `isAllowed`, `roundCzk`, `formatCzk`, `formatPct`.
   - **`public/js/app.js`:**
     - `track()` beacon (once per event per page; `page_view` on load)
     - dialogs `openDialog(el, cls)` (focuses the first input) / `closeDialog`, focus trap + Esc
     - menu; calculator `renderResult`
     - IntersectionObserver for `result_view`
     - "Jak počítáme" rendering
     - form validation with `F = form.elements` and the regexes `EMAIL_RE`, `PHONE_RE = /^\+?[0-9 ]{9,16}$/`
     - submit POSTs `/api/lead` with `{name, email, help, phone, tips, company, elapsedMs, monthly, years, variant, device, pid, utm_*}`
     - consent `readConsent`/`saveConsent`/`loadTrackers`; `has-banner` on `document.documentElement`
   - **`public/js/config.js`:** `TRACKERS = { ga4: '', metaPixel: '' }`.
   - **`public/navod.html`, `public/ochrana-udaju.html`, `public/css/page.css`:** the guide (6 steps, verified tax facts, ČNB seznamy link) and the privacy/cookies page (operator details as `[placeholders]`). Also `public/favicon.svg`, `public/og.png`, `public/email/logo.png`.
   - **`api/lead.js`:**
     - validate (spam → silent 200; errors → 400)
     - rate limit 5/h per hashed IP via `ipKey(req)` (exported)
     - `store.addLead`
     - `sendMail` with the comparison e-mail, plus a team e-mail if `help && TEAM_EMAIL`
     - mail failure → 502 + `lead_error`; success → `lead_ok` + `{ok:true}`
   - **`api/event.js`:** whitelisted events (excluding the server-only `lead_ok`/`lead_error`); rate limit 300/h; `incrEvent`; 204.
   - **`api/stats.js`:** `aggregate(raw)` → byVariant/byDevice/byAd/byDay/total/conversion; returns 403 in production without the token; JSON, or HTML with `format=html`.
   - **`lib/store.js`:** `FUNNEL`/`OTHER_EVENTS`/`EVENTS`, Redis store (pipeline `hincrby` on `ev:`/`evd:`/`evc:`, `sadd days`, `lpush leads`, rate limit `incr`+`expire`) and memory store (`_reset`, `_leads`); `getStore()`.
   - **`lib/mailer.js`:** Brevo fetch; without a key it pushes to `getOutbox()` and writes `mail-outbox/*.html` locally.
   - **`lib/validate.js`:** `validateLead` (honeypot / `MIN_FILL_MS` 2000 → spam; name, email, phone required only when help is on; allowed inputs; sanitised utm).
   - **`lib/email-template.js`:** `renderComparisonEmail({monthly, years, help, phone})` → {subject, html, text, preheader}, and `renderTeamEmail(lead)`. Latest changes:
     - the logo is now `<img src="${logoUrl()}" width="120" height="28" alt="veenos" style="display:block;border:0;...">` with `const logoUrl = () => \`${site()}/email/logo.png\`` (`site()` uses `SITE_URL`, default `https://veenos.vercel.app`)
     - the breakdown is now stacked rows: ticker + total on one line, then "Poplatek fondu X (TER ročně) · Nákup z ČR Y (provize + směna)", then issuer · listing · broker
     - a caption "Celkem = poplatek fondu + nákup z Česka (provize a směna měn)"
     - `nowrap` on amounts
   - **`tests/`:** `model.test.js`, `lead.test.js`, `email.test.js`; 15 tests pass (`npm test` → `node --test`).
   - **`ads/`:** `ad.css` (inlined @font-face with relative paths, `.h` line-height 1.12), `ad-a.html` (VOO = CSPX card), `ad-b.html` ("Kupujete S&P 500?" + 4 bars, CTA "Porovnat 4 fondy →"), `og.html`, `email-logo.html`, `png/*`, `README.md` (Slovak ad copy with UTM `utm_source=meta&utm_medium=paid_social&utm_campaign=sp500_srovnani&utm_content=ad_a|ad_b`).
   - **Config files:** `vercel.json`, `package.json` (deps `@upstash/redis`, dev `@fontsource/manrope`), `.env.example`, `.claude/launch.json` (veenos-dev, port 3000).
   - **`.gitignore`:** `.claude/settings.local.json`, `__pycache__/`, `node_modules/`, `.env`, `.env.local`, `.vercel/`, `mail-outbox/`, `.edge-render-profile/`, `.ai-log-redact`, `.vercel`, `.env*.local`.
   - **`README.md` (Slovak):**
     - links (Stránka https://veenos.vercel.app, repo)
     - target group, offer/ask table, section order
     - ads with images, data/sources table, measurement
     - quality verification (Lighthouse 96/100/100/100, LCP 2.3 s, CLS 0; 15 tests; production test result and the 2 e-mail bugs fixed)
     - expected conversion 4–8 % (centre ~6 %; funnel table 3.4/8.1/12.0 %; CPL ~130–200 Kč)
     - 3 A/B hypotheses: (1) partial result + gate, (2) variant B, (3) e-mail only
     - the AI workflow and errors (including the e-mail bugs found only by the live test)
     - decisions on ambiguities; what's missing (including brevosend.com sender); structure; how to run

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Agents hit the session limit:** resumed them with SendMessage after the reset.
   - **Placeholder git identity:** set a repo-local name and the noreply e-mail.
   - **`node --test tests/` failed on Node 22:** test script changed to `node --test`.
   - **`form.name` shadowing:** replaced with `form.elements[...]`.
   - **Dialog focused the close button:** the selector now prefers inputs.
   - **Headless Edge async screenshot (files appeared late):** `waitFor` loop plus a separate profile.
   - **Ads rendered in a fallback font:** relative @font-face; layout overlaps fixed by resizing; "zdarma" in an ad removed (the user had earlier said "zdarma" "will not be true").
   - **Empty inline script committed after a sed failure:** rewrote `scripts/inline_v12_preview.py`.
   - **Preview pane `data:` URLs broke relative iframes:** inlined via srcdoc + `window.name` presets.
   - **User feedback "mobile seems smaller than v11":** caused by the 66 % preview scale and the 320 px mock; fixed with clamp sizing and an 82 % preview scale.
   - **Tablet cookie card covered the CTA:** padding-bottom 236px.
   - **"á" collision in the headline (user feedback):** line-height 1.12 and 42px.
   - **Lighthouse CLS 0.102 and contrast:** banner state moved to the head script on the `html` class; `.asof` colour #9a9b96. After the fix: CLS 0, a11y 100.
   - **E-mail missing logo (Gmail strips SVG `data:` URIs):** hosted PNG `public/email/logo.png`.
   - **E-mail breakdown overlap (user screenshot):** stacked rows.
   - **ai-log duplicated per date:** `session_start` naming; stale 10-11 files removed.
   - **Vercel GitHub connect error:** deploys via CLI only.
   - **PSI API quota exceeded:** ran Lighthouse locally.

5. Problem Solving:
   - Resolved the NYSE vs PRIIPs tension: compare SPY/IVV/VOO plus the CSPX UCITS twin.
   - After the user caught drift from the brief, the comparison was re-centred (concept A, S&P 500 only).
   - Fairness assumption: quarterly Fio orders.
   - Production verified: page 200, headers, `/api/stats` 403 without token, events 204, the real lead flow through the UI succeeded, and the e-mail was delivered (DKIM/SPF/DMARC pass, From `…@brevosend.com`, Reply-To the user's Gmail, landed in Gmail Updates).
   - Environment variables present: BREVO_API_KEY, MAIL_FROM, STATS_TOKEN, SITE_URL, KV_*/REDIS_URL. TEAM_EMAIL is not set.

Gmail grouped it into the same conversation as the first e-mail because the subject is the same: "Vaše srovnání S&P 500 ETF: CSPX vychází nejlevněji". Open the last message in that conversation.

I can see the e-mail arrived and that the logo is now an image rather than text, but I can't see how Gmail displays it. Please check two things:

- the "Z čeho se náklady skládají" section has each fund on its own block, with no overlapping text

```
const BAR_MS = 1600, STAGGER_MS = 140;
const ease = t => 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
// Bars grow and amounts count up together, once the bars are on screen (on phones the page is still scrolling to them).

    const step = now => {
      const t = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (now - start) / BAR_MS));
      num.textContent = formatCzk(Math.round(ease(t) * to / 100) * 100);
      if (t < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(step);
  });
}
```

```
("  .hero-bg { position: absolute; left: 0; right: 0; top: 0; height: 100svh; min-height: 600px; overflow: hidden; }",
 "  /* Phone: the photo is a band above the headline (a shorter box scales the photo down, so the landmark sits above the text). */\n  .hero-bg { position: absolute; left: 0; right: 0; top: 0; height: clamp(380px, 56svh, 520px); overflow: hidden; }"),
("  .scrim { position: absolute; inset: 0; background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(24,25,28,.32) 0%, rgba(24,25,28,.32) 28%, rgba(24,25,28,.88) 60%, var(--bg) 100%); }",

EOF
echo done
```

**→ Bash** — Write an edge-overflow scan script

```
mkdir -p "/c/Users/matus/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Users-matus-Desktop-B-project/66014a9f-a5a6-413b-8552-f9844a0020a6/scratchpad" && cat > "/c/Users/matus/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Users-matus-Desktop-B-project/66014a9f-a5a6-413b-8552-f9844a0020a6/scratchpad/edge-scan.js" <<'EOF'
(() => {
  const W = document.documentElement.clientWidth, M = 12, out = [];
  const skip = el => el.closest('.menu, .sheet, .cs, .dim, .cdim, .hero-bg, .sr-only, .hp');
  const walker = document.createTreeWalker(document.body, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
  let n;
  while ((n = walker.nextNode())) {
    if (!n.textContent.trim()) continue;
    const el = n.parentElement;
    if (!el || skip(el)) continue;
    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.visibility === 'hidden' || el.offsetParent === null && cs.position !== 'fixed') continue;
    const r = document.createRange(); r.selectNodeContents(n);
    for (const b of r.getClientRects()) {
      if (b.width && (b.left < M || b.right > W - M)) { out.push(`${el.tagName}.${el.className} "${n.textContent.trim().slice(0, 30)}" L${b.left.toFixed(0)} R${(W - b.right).toFixed(0)}`); break; }
    }
  }
  return { W, hscroll: document.documentElement.scrollWidth > W, issues: [...new Set(out)] };
})()
EOF
echo saved
```

```
## Round 14 — 2026-10-11 (post-build design tweaks)

| # | Decision | Change | Why / implications |

| D23 | Calculator button | **"Porovnat 4 fondy" is always full lime** (no greyed-out disabled state). If an amount or the years are missing, that label turns lime, its chips nudge and focus moves to them. | Human: looks better without the greyed-out effect. The hint keeps the "what's missing" signal without a dead-looking button. |
| D24 | Hero position (phone + tablet) | Headline starts in the **upper part of the first screen** (≈ 23 % on a 390×844 phone) instead of being pinned to the bottom of a full-height block; the calculator card now peeks into the first screen. Photo is a shorter band above the headline (Prague Castle / face stay visible above the text). Top padding shrinks on short phones so the CTA stays above the cookie card; the hero no longer moves when the banner closes. | Human: half the mobile screen was empty. |
| D25 | Edge bleed | Fixed "Jak počítáme" touching the screen edges on phones (a section rule reset the page's side padding). Checked text-to-edge distance ≥ 12 px at 320, 375, 390, 430, 552, 700, 768, 1100, 1280, 1440 px. | Human screenshot. |
| D26 | Result animation | Bars grow over **1.6 s, staggered**, and the amounts **count up** with them; starts when the bars are on screen. Screen readers get the final amount only; reduced-motion users get no animation. | Human request: slower, with counting numbers. |
| D27 | Contrast | Cards and chips one step lighter (`#25262b` / `#2e2f35`, border `#34353b`), footer darker (`#0e0f11`) with its own top border. Same tokens on the subpages. | Human request: footer and cards more distinct. |
EOF
git add -A && git status --short
```

```
Design tweaks: hero higher, always-lit compare button, counting result, more contrast

- Hero text starts in the upper part of the first screen on phone/tablet;
  the photo is a shorter band above the headline; calculator peeks in.
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-10-11 13:02:11

All five tweaks are live on https://veenos.vercel.app and pushed to GitHub.

- **"Porovnat 4 fondy" button:** it's always fully lit now. If someone taps it without choosing an amount or the number of years, the missing label turns lime, those chips give a small shake, and focus moves to them.
- **Hero on mobile:** the headline now starts about a quarter of the way down the screen instead of near the middle. The photo is a shorter band above it, so Prague Castle (variant A) and the face (variant B) sit above the text instead of behind it. The calculator card now peeks into the first screen. On small phones like the iPhone SE, the "Začít investovat" button still clears the cookie banner. Tablet got the same treatment.
- **Text at the screen edge:** the cause was the "Jak počítáme" section. On phones a section rule wiped out its side margins, which is what your second screenshot shows. I checked every width from 320 px to 1440 px: no text is closer than 12 px to the edge and nothing scrolls sideways.
- **Result animation:** the bars now grow over about 2 seconds, one after another, and the amounts count up alongside them. The animation starts only once the bars are on screen, which matters on phones because the page is still scrolling down to them. Screen readers announce only the final amount, and people who turn off animations in their phone settings see the result straight away.
- **Contrast:** the calculator, cards and chips are a shade lighter, and the footer is noticeably darker with its own top border. The návod and ochrana údajů pages use the same colours.

Lighthouse on mobile, after the changes: performance 98, accessibility 100, best practices 100, SEO 100. Page load (LCP) is 2.4 s and layout shift (CLS) is 0.001. My first run showed a layout-shift problem, but it had caught the old version of the page while the deploy was still going live; the repeat run was clean.

I updated those numbers in the README and logged the five changes as round 14 in `research/decisions.md`.

```
function routeParams(fund) {
  const r = ROUTES[fund.route];
  return { fx: r.fx, commissionCzk: r.commissionUsd * ASSUMPTIONS.usdCzk, ordersEveryMonths: r.ordersEveryMonths };
}

// Total costs in CZK per fund, cheapest first.
// buyCost depends only on the route (identical for SPY/IVV/VOO); feeCost is the rest of the total.
export function compare(monthly, years) {
  const base = futureValue(monthly, years);
  return FUNDS.map(f => {
    const route = routeParams(f);
    const total = base - futureValue(monthly, years, { ter: f.ter, ...route });
    const buyCost = base - futureValue(monthly, years, route);
    return { ticker: f.ticker, total, feeCost: total - buyCost, buyCost, fund: f, route: ROUTES[f.route] };
  }).sort((a, b) => a.total - b.total || a.ticker.localeCompare(b.ticker));
}

export const isAllowed = (monthly, years) => ALLOWED.monthly.includes(monthly) && ALLOWED.years.includes(years);
```

```
});
```

```
27:## 3. Poradie sekcií a prečo
28:1. **Hero:** „Stejný index. Jiná cena." + 1 veta + 3 krátke odrážky + 1 CTA. Nadväzuje na obe reklamy (message match +39–48 % v dvoch testoch, log C10) a prejde 2-sekundovým testom: čo to je, čo z toho mám, kam kliknúť. Na desktope je kalkulačka priamo v hero vedľa nadpisu.
35:Cookie lišta sa ukáže pri prvej návšteve (mobil: karta, tablet: stred, desktop: lišta cez celú šírku). Kým je viditeľná, hero sa posunie, aby CTA nezakrývala.
64:`page_view → hero_cta → calc_select → calc_compare → result_view → form_open → form_start → help_on → form_submit → lead_ok / lead_error` (+ `consent_accept/reject`)
93:| 2 | **Variant B (mladý muž + „Spočítat poplatky")** zvýši preklik z hero oproti A (Pražský hrad + „Začít investovat"). „Začít investovat" sľubuje otvorenie účtu, ale klik otvorí kalkulačku. | `hero_cta / page_view`, `calc_compare / page_view` | Prvá obrazovka rozhoduje o všetkom ďalšom. Pozor: B mení fotku aj CTA naraz, je to test celej „mladšej" varianty a nepovie, ktorý z dvoch prvkov zabral. | ~1 000 / variant |
102:1. **Plán bez rozhodnutí.** Najprv plán výskumu; rozhodnutia o segmente, magnete a dizajne mali prísť až z dát a spolu so mnou.
```

**→ Bash** — Read the section-order list

```
sed -n 28,36p README.md
```

<details><summary>result</summary>

```
1. **Hero:** „Stejný index. Jiná cena." + 1 veta + 3 krátke odrážky + 1 CTA. Nadväzuje na obe reklamy (message match +39–48 % v dvoch testoch, log C10) a prejde 2-sekundovým testom: čo to je, čo z toho mám, kam kliknúť. Na desktope je kalkulačka priamo v hero vedľa nadpisu.
2. **Kalkulačka:** len 2 voľby (mesačne, roky) na jednej karte, žiadne písanie. Podľa zadania sú interaktívne prvky cestou ku kontaktu.
3. **Výsledok:** 4 pruhy na jednej škále, najlacnejší zvýraznený. Toto je samotné **porovnanie ETF z NYSE**, ktoré zadanie žiada, s európskym dvojčaťom ako kontrolou z pohľadu Čecha.
4. **Formulár (bottom sheet / dialóg):** až po výsledku, 2 povinné polia, autofill.
5. **Jak počítáme:** zdroje a predpoklady pre skeptikov, pod výsledkom, aby nebrzdili. Počíta sa z rovnakých dát ako kalkulačka.
6. **Pätička:** upozornenie (nejde o investičné odporúčanie), ochrana údajov, nastavenie cookies.

Cookie lišta sa ukáže pri prvej návšteve (mobil: karta, tablet: stred, desktop: lišta cez celú šírku). Kým je viditeľná, hero sa posunie, aby CTA nezakrývala.
```

</details>

**→ Bash** — Update README section order and footer notes

```
python - <<'EOF'
p='README.md'
s=open(p,encoding='utf-8').read()
rep=[
("Na desktope je kalkulačka priamo v hero vedľa nadpisu.\n",
 "Na desktope je kalkulačka priamo v hero vedľa nadpisu.\n"
 "   - **Faktický pás na konci prvej obrazovky (mobil, tablet):** „3 000 Kč měsíčně · 20 let → **114 200 Kč** rozdíl v nákladech mezi nejlevnějším a nejdražším fondem na stejný index S&P 500.“ Jedno reálne číslo z modelu (SPY 142 100 − CSPX 27 900), ktoré dokazuje sľub „Jiná cena“ ešte pred prvým klikom a zároveň uzatvára prvú obrazovku, takže kalkulačka príde až po scrollnutí. Test v `tests/model.test.js` stráži, aby sa číslo v HTML nerozišlo s modelom.\n"),
("6. **Pätička:** upozornenie (nejde o investičné odporúčanie), ochrana údajov, nastavenie cookies.",
 "6. **Pätička:** kontakt (fiktívny: +420 715 123 456, fake@nonreal.cz; položka „Kontakt“ v menu sem skočí), upozornenie (nejde o investičné odporúčanie), ochrana údajov, nastavenie cookies."),
("Kým je viditeľná, hero sa posunie, aby CTA nezakrývala.",
 "Hero je navrhnuté tak, aby CTA zostalo nad lištou aj na malom telefóne (375 × 667)."),
]
for a,b in rep:
    assert s.count(a)==1,a[:50]; s=s.replace(a,b)
open(p,'w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\n').write(s)
EOF
grep -n "testy\|15 test\|16 test" README.md | head
```

```
97:\* Hrubý odhad pre 80 % silu a α = 0,05 (n ≈ 16·p(1−p)/δ²). Celková konverzia 6 % → +20 % relatívne by vyžadovala ~6 300 návštev na variant, preto sa testy vyhodnocujú na úrovni konkrétneho kroku funnelu. Varianta stránky sa dá vynútiť cez `?v=a|b` (napr. z reklamy), inak 50/50 pri každom načítaní.
```

**→ Bash** — Log decisions, commit and deploy

```
sed -i 's/- \*\*Testy:\*\* 15 automatických testov/- **Testy:** 16 automatických testov/' README.md && cat >> research/decisions.md <<'EOF'

## Round 15 — 2026-10-11 (first screen, chips, contact)

| # | Decision | Change | Why / implications |
|---|---|---|---|
| D28 | First screen (phone + tablet) | The calculator no longer peeks into the first screen. A **fact band** closes it: "3 000 Kč měsíčně · 20 let" + ⓘ, **"114 200 Kč"** in lime, "Rozdíl v nákladech mezi nejlevnějším a nejdražším fondem na stejný index S&P 500." The hero block is one screen tall; the calculator starts at the fold. Desktop unchanged (calculator stays next to the headline). | Human request ("a % number or a marketing line"). A CZK figure over a percentage: it matches the hero promise "Poplatky v korunách, ne v procentech". The number is SPY − CSPX from `compare(3000, 20)` (142 100 − 27 900); a test fails if HTML and model drift apart. Neutral wording: a fact, not a recommendation. |
| D29 | Chips | Tapping the selected chip again **deselects it**. | Human request. |
| D30 | Contact | **"Kontakt"** in the header nav and the mobile menu → contact block in the footer (`#kontakt`): +420 715 123 456, fake@nonreal.cz (fictional, showcase). Also linked from the subpages and used in the privacy policy as the controller contact. | Human request; fictional details supplied by the human. |
EOF
git add -A && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF'
First-screen fact band, chip deselect, contact

- Phone/tablet: hero is one screen tall and ends with a fact band
  (114 200 Kč = SPY − CSPX for 3 000 Kč/20 let); the calculator starts
  below the fold. Test keeps the number in sync with the model.
- Tapping a selected chip again clears it.
- "Kontakt" in nav + menu, contact block in the footer (fictional
  +420 715 123 456, fake@nonreal.cz), linked from the subpages.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
git log --oneline -1 && vercel deploy --prod --yes 2>&1 | grep -i "error\|aliased\|production" | head -3
```

All three changes are live on https://veenos.vercel.app and pushed to GitHub.

**1. Calculator no longer in the first screen (mobile and tablet).** I went with a money figure rather than a percentage, because the hero already promises "Poplatky v korunách, ne v procentech". The bottom of the first screen now shows:

> 3 000 Kč měsíčně · 20 let ⓘ
> **114 200 Kč** (in lime)
> Rozdíl v nákladech mezi nejlevnějším a nejdražším fondem na stejný index S&P 500.

- **Source:** it's a real result from our calculator (SPY 142 100 Kč minus CSPX 27 900 Kč). The ⓘ links to "Jak počítáme".
- **Test:** a new test fails if the number on the page ever stops matching the calculator. That brings the total to 16 tests, all passing.
- **Placement checked:** the calculator starts exactly at the bottom edge of the screen at 320×640, 375×667, 390×844 and 768×1024, so it only appears after scrolling.
- **Cookie banner:** it covers this band on a first visit, but the main button stays above the banner even on small phones.
- **Desktop:** unchanged; the calculator stays next to the headline there.

If you'd rather have the percentage, the same comparison works out to "o 80 % nižší náklady".

**2. Deselect.** Tapping a selected amount or number of years again clears it. If someone then presses "Porovnat 4 fondy", the missing choice is highlighted as before.

**3. Kontakt.**
- **Where it is:** a new item in the desktop header and the phone menu. It jumps to a contact block in the footer with +420 715 123 456 (tap to call) and fake@nonreal.cz (tap to e-mail).
- **Subpages:** the footers of the návod and ochrana údajů pages also link to it.
- **Privacy page:** I put the same fake e-mail and phone into the privacy policy, where the contact was still a "[e-mail]" placeholder. The company name, IČO and address there are still placeholders.

The README now covers the new band and the contact block, and I logged these three changes in `research/decisions.md`.

```
# Conversion & psychology evidence for a mobile finance lead-gen landing page

All sources accessed 2026-10-10. Tags: `VERIFIED` = I opened the page (or extracted the PDF) and it states this; `INFERRED` = my reasoning, or I only saw a search-result snippet / secondary summary. Evidence strength: **Strong** = peer-reviewed, large-sample experiment or multi-company dataset; **Medium** = credible industry dat

## Summary
- Finance-specific and Czech-specific evidence is thin. Most lead-gen CRO numbers come from vendors or agencies. The most solid material is general behavioural research (peer-reviewed) plus a few large datasets (Unbounce, Google/Deloitte, GetResponse).
- Benchmark: Unbounce's financial-services landing page median is 8.3%. Investing is much lower (3.9%). Paid social in finance is 9.3% (Facebook 10.1%, TikTok 10.8%, Instagram 15.5%). Finance converts better on mobile (11.5%) than desktop (9%). These are medians over pages that had conversions, so they are probably optimistic.
- No controlled study was found on where to put the email gate for a calculator, quiz or personalised result. Vendor claims point in opposite directions. The claim "interactive converts 2x" traces
… [truncated, 24214 more chars]
```

```
## Options & trade-offs (input only, no recommendation)
- **Gate timing.** (a) Upfront: maximum commitment signal but vendor claims say more drop-off. (b) After a partial result/preview: supported by IKEA/endowed-progress logic, untested for leads. (c) Optional skip: more readers, unknown quality (Act-On snippet). (d) After full result: best trust per vendors, risk of free riders.
- **Form shape.** Email-only (fewest fields, no personalisation) vs first name + email (small friction, enables salutation) vs two-step (some case-study gains, vendor-heavy evidence). Phone field: lower volume, possibly higher intent, evidence anecdotal.
- **Consent.** Unticked required checkbox (compliant, about -15% in one test) vs consent text next to the button vs double opt-in (about 4x fewer subscribers in GetResponse data but better open and click). Legal check needed.
- **Lead-magnet format.** Calculator, comparison tool, quiz, report or email course: all have vendor-only conversion claims; completion burden differs (short quiz vs multi-field calculator).
- **Fee framing.** TER % (familiar, abstract) vs CZK over 20 years (concrete, could be seen as scare tactic) vs both. Evidence supports salience
```

```
## 6. Meranie celej cesty
Funnel sa meria **bez cookies**: prehliadač posiela udalosti cez `sendBeacon`, prepojené len ID v pamäti stránky. Na server sa neukladá IP adresa, iba denné počty podľa varianty, zariadenia a reklamy (`utm_content`).

`page_view → hero_cta → calc_select → calc_compare → result_view → form_open → form_start → help_on → form_submit → lead_ok / lead_error` (+ `consent_accept/reject`)

Prehľad je na `/api/stats?token=…&format=html`: funnel podľa A/B varianty, zariadenia a reklamy plus počet leadov. GA4 a Meta Pixel sú pripravené v [`public/js/config.js`](public/js/config.js), načítajú sa iba po súhlase a v tejto ukážke sú vypnuté (prázdne ID). Cookie lišta je súčasťou ukážky.
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-10-11 13:12:44

Overall, the brief is essentially done: every requirement is met in substance, and the gaps are about launching for real, not about the assignment. The four places where we deviated from the research were your deliberate calls, and they're documented.

## 1. What the task asked for vs what we built

| Task requirement | What we built | Status |
|---|---|---|
| Comparison of ETFs traded on NYSE | SPY, IVV and VOO (all listed on NYSE Arca) plus CSPX, the European fund on the same index that Czechs can actually buy, all costed in Kč | ✅ but S&P 500 only |
| Czech visitor from a mobile ad, a few seconds of attention | Mobile-first page, one statement per screen, "vy", page loads in 2.4 s | ✅ |
| Value in exchange for the contact, visible before the form | 4-fund cost result shown before the form; the 114 200 Kč fact band is on the first screen | ✅ |
| Define target group, the offer, and when we ask | README §1–2: a do-it-yourself investor who knows the S&P 500; we ask after the result | ✅ |
| Must not look like default Claude output | Own brand (Veenos), 15 design rounds with you | ✅ (subjective) |
| Two ad versions; first screen continues the ad | A: comparison card, B: question hook. Both in feed and story formats, with copy and tracking links. Hero headline = ad A headline | ✅ |
| Interactive elements lead to the contact | One calculator → result → "Poslat srovnání e-mailem" | ✅ |
| As few form fields as possible | Name + e-mail required; phone only if "Chci pomoct" is on | ⚠️ e-mail only would be the true minimum |
| Think through what happens after submit | Confirmation on the page, a detailed e-mail (tested in your Gmail), optional call | ✅ no follow-up e-mails |
| Real data with sources | Fund issuers, ČNB, Fio and XTB price lists, each with a date | ✅ Fio's currency-exchange markup is approximate |
| Measure the whole journey | 11 steps from arrival to submit, split by page variant, device and ad, at `/api/stats` | ✅ but Meta gets no conversion signal |
| README: section order, expected conversion, 3 ranked A/B hypotheses, how AI was used, decisions, what's missing | All present; expected conversion 4–8 % | ✅ |
| Free hosting, public repo with history | Vercel + GitHub | ✅ |
| `ai-log` with `/export` transcripts | Our script exports automatically, but the official `/export` hasn't been run yet | ⚠️ you need to run it |
| "Could we launch ads tomorrow?" | The page works end to end. Blockers: real company details plus a legal check, Meta's rules for unlicensed advertisers, own e-mail domain, Pixel ID, paid Vercel plan | ⚠️ |

## 2. What the research suggested vs what we used

The research agents were told to give options, not decisions. Where they did lean one way, this is how it compares:

| Area | Research suggested | What we used | Match |
|---|---|---|---|
| Main angle | Lead agent recommended "VOO si u většiny brokerů nekoupíte…" (most Czech brokers won't sell it to you) | Neutral "Stejný index. Jiná cena." | Your override |
| Target group | Do-it-yourself investor fits best | Same | ✅ |
| What they get | Personal cost result in Kč (only Portu offers anything similar) | 4-fund cost comparison | ✅ |
| When we ask | After the interaction, with part of the result visible (Portu, Fondee and Finax do this) | Same | ✅ |
| Interactive part | Calculator plus a table pairing each NYSE fund with its European equivalent | Calculator only | Your override (simpler) |
| Form | E-mail only; a required consent checkbox cost 15 % of sign-ups in one test | Name + e-mail, phone opt-in, unticked tips box, autofill | ⚠️ one extra field; consent ✅ |
| How costs are shown | Kč over many years rather than %, because people ignore small amounts | Kč over 5–30 years, "v korunách, ne v procentech" | ✅ |
| Ad matching the page | +39–48 % in tests, which reused the page's image in the ad | Same headline, colours and fonts; the ads don't reuse the hero photo | ⚠️ partial |
| Tone | "Vy", plain language, no fake urgency, no "nejlepší" | Same | ✅ |
| Trust | Regulator + client count, ČNB as an anchor (77 % of Czechs trust it), real contact | "Data od emitentů a ČNB", sources; contact is fictional; no regulator or client claims possible | ⚠️ thin |
| Speed | Page load under 2.5 s | 2.4 s | ✅ (narrowly) |
| Cookie banner | It covers the first screen at every competitor; "reject" must be in the first layer | Small card, reject button in the first layer, CTA stays visible | ✅ better than competitors |
| Measurement | Czech regulators may require consent even for analytics without cookies | Counters without consent; GA4 and Pixel only after consent (currently off) | ⚠️ README doesn't flag this legal uncertainty |
| After submit | Welcome e-mails perform well; consider a second step on the thank-you screen | One instant e-mail and a confirmation | ⚠️ |
| Scope | Started as 3 index families | S&P 500 only (your later refocus on the brief's core) | Your override |

## How close to perfect

- **Against the brief:** about 90 %. Everything is covered; the open items are the field count, the `/export` you still need to run, and the "launch tomorrow" blockers.
- **Against the research:** about 80 %. All the main levers are in: costs in Kč, asking after value, the headline matching the ad, speed, "vy", clean consent. The four deviations were your calls and are documented.
- **For a real launch tomorrow:** about 70 %. What's left is mostly business and legal setup, not code.

Biggest gaps, ordered by expected effect on conversions:
1. **No Meta Pixel ID**, so Meta can't optimise the ads for leads. Adding one is about an hour of work once you have the ID.
2. **Thin trust:** no real company and no social proof. That's built into it being a showcase.
3. **Ads don't reuse the hero photo**, which is what the tested ad-to-page lift relied on.
4. **No follow-up** after the first e-mail.
5. **The name field**, which is worth an A/B test on its own.

One gap I can close right now is adding the measurement-consent uncertainty to the README's "Čo chýba" section; it's currently missing. I can also turn these two tables into a shareable page if you want to send them along.

```
13:- **Czech tax correction:** the 40 million CZK cap applied to securities **only for 2025** and was abolished from 1 Jan 2026 (Finanční správa, 5 Jan 2026). It remains for crypto. The 3-year time test and the 100 000 CZK proceeds test remain. **[CHANGED 2024-26]**
14:- **Dividends:** US withholding is 15% with a W-8BEN (IRS treaty table, rate also covers RIC/ETF dividends), 30% without. Foreign dividends can be put in a separate 15% base (§8(8) ZDP); foreign tax is credited (§38f).
```

```
import os, re
os.chdir(r'C:\Users\matus\Desktop\B_project')

p = 'README.md'
s = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()
lines = s.split('\n')
i = next(k for k, l in enumerate(lines) if l.startswith('   - **Faktický pás na konci prvej obrazovky'))
lines[i] = ('   - **Faktický pás na konci prvej obrazovky (mobil, tablet):** „3 000 Kč měsíčně · 20 let → **o 80 % nižší náklady** '
            'u nejlevnějšího ze 4 fondů na stejný index S&P 500 oproti nejdražšímu (27 900 Kč vs 142 100 Kč).“ '
            'Reálne číslo z modelu, ktoré dokazuje sľub „Jiná cena“ ešte pred prvým klikom a zároveň uzatvára prvú obrazovku, '
            'takže kalkulačka príde až po scrollnutí. Percento je nadpis, korunové sumy v podtexte držia sľub „v korunách“. '
            'Test v `tests/model.test.js` stráži, aby sa text v HTML nerozišiel s modelom.')
s = '\n'.join(lines)
a = '6. **Pätička:**'
assert s.count(a) == 1
s = s.replace(a, '6. **Časté otázky (5):** prečo väčšinu ETF z NYSE u českého brokera nekúpite, čím sa líšia SPY/IVV/VOO/CSPX, dane, či ide o investičné odporúčanie a čo bude s kontaktom. Odpovedajú na námietky, ktoré by inak brzdili odoslanie formulára; fakty sú z overeného výskumu (`research/05-data-facts.md`). Otvorenie otázky sa meria (`faq_open`).\n7. **Pätička:**')
open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write(s)

p = 'research/decisions.md'
s = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()
s += """
## Round 16 — 2026-10-11 (fact band copy, footer, FAQ)

| # | Decision | Change | Why / implications |
|---|---|---|---|
| D28b | Fact band copy (revises D28) | Headline is now the percentage: **"o 80 % nižší náklady"** (only "80 %" in lime), smaller type (≈ 32 px phone, 42 px tablet). Sub-line keeps the CZK figures: "u nejlevnějšího ze 4 fondů na stejný index S&P 500 oproti nejdražšímu (27 900 Kč vs 142 100 Kč)." | Human choice. The CZK figures in the sub-line keep the hero promise "Poplatky v korunách, ne v procentech". 80 % = 1 − 27 900 / 142 100; the test checks percentage and both amounts against the model. |
| D31 | Meta Pixel / GA4 | **Stay off** in the showcase; no IDs. | Human: showcase only. The consent-gated loaders remain as a demonstration. |
| D32 | Footer layout | Links are **top-aligned and never wrap**; one column from tablet up (was a 2-column grid whose stretched rows centred the "Nastavení cookies" button and made a zig-zag). | Human screenshot. |
| D33 | FAQ | **5 questions** after "Jak počítáme": NYSE access (PRIIPs/KID), SPY/IVV/VOO vs CSPX, taxes, "is it advice?", what happens with my contact. One open at a time; the card height grows with a grid-rows transition, the + springs into an × on a lime disc, a soft lime glow lights the card, and the answer fades up out of a blur. Reduced-motion users get no animation. Linked from the menu and footer; opens are counted (`faq_open`). | Human request. Answers use only verified facts (05-data-facts, verification log E1/E2/E6) and avoid duplicating the numbers that live in the model. |
"""
open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write(s)
print('ok')
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-10-11 17:40:53

All of it is live on https://veenos.vercel.app and pushed to GitHub.

**Fact band:** the headline is now "o **80 %** nižší náklady" in smaller type, with only "80 %" in lime. Underneath: "u nejlevnějšího ze 4 fondů na stejný index S&P 500 oproti nejdražšímu (27 900 Kč vs 142 100 Kč)". I kept the Kč amounts there because the hero promises "Poplatky v korunách, ne v procentech", and a percentage on its own would contradict that. The calculator still starts below the fold on phone and tablet.

**Meta Pixel:** nothing added, as you asked.

**Footer zig-zag:** fixed on tablet and desktop. The links had been in a two-column grid where the rows stretched, so "Nastavení cookies" got centred vertically in its cell and wrapped onto two lines. From tablet up they're now one straight column with even spacing, and no link wraps.

**FAQ section:** five questions, placed after "Jak počítáme":
1. Proč většinu ETF z NYSE u českého brokera nekoupíte?
2. Čím se liší SPY, IVV, VOO a CSPX?
3. Jak je to s daněmi?
4. Je to investiční doporučení?
5. Co mi pošlete a co bude s mým kontaktem?

The answers only use facts we checked during the research. Fund fees aren't repeated there; the answers link to "Jak počítáme" instead, so the numbers can't get out of sync.

When a question opens:
- the card grows smoothly
- the "+" springs into an "×" on a lime circle
- a soft lime glow lights the card's border
- the answer fades up out of a blur

Opening another question closes the current one. People who turn off animations on their phone get no animation.

"Časté otázky" is also in the phone menu and the footer, and FAQ opens now show in `/api/stats`.

I checked phone, tablet and desktop: nothing touches the screen edges, there are no console errors, and 16/16 tests pass. I also updated the README section order and added these changes to `research/decisions.md`.

```
import os, re
os.chdir(r'C:\Users\matus\Desktop\B_project')
p = 'public/index.html'
s = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()

def rep(a, b):
    global s
    n = s.count(a)
    assert n == 1, (n, a[:90])
    s = s.replace(a, b)

# brighter cards with a clearer border, solid colours only
rep("  .qa { position: relative; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 20px; background: var(--card); overflow: hidden; isolation: isolate; transition: border-color .45s, box-shadow .6s cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1); }\n",
    "  .qa { border: 1px solid #3c3d43; border-radius: 20px; background: #2c2d33; overflow: hidden; transition: border-color .45s, background .45s, box-shadow .6s cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1); }\n"
    "  .qa:hover { border-color: #55565c; }\n")
rep("  .qa::before { content: \"\"; position: absolute; inset: 0; z-index: -1; background: radial-gradient(420px 220px at 0 0, rgba(207,230,138,.13), transparent 70%); opacity: 0; transition: opacity .6s; }\n", "")
rep("  .qa.open { border-color: rgba(207,230,138,.38); box-shadow: 0 22px 44px -28px rgba(207,230,138,.45); }\n",
    "  .qa.open { border-color: rgba(207,230,138,.6); background: #303137; box-shadow: 0 18px 40px -22px rgba(0,0,0,.7); }\n")
rep("  .qa.open::before { opacity: 1; }\n", "")
# no numbering
rep("  .qa-n { font-size: 12px; font-weight: 800; color: #6f706b; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; flex: none; width: 20px; transition: color .4s; }\n", "")
rep("  .qa.open .qa-n { color: var(--accent); }\n", "")
rep(" .qa-n { width: 24px; }", "")
s = re.sub(r'<span class="qa-n" aria-hidden="true">0\d</span>', '', s)
assert 'qa-n' not in s
# padding: the answer now lines up with the question text
rep("padding: 18px 16px 18px 18px; background: none;", "padding: 18px 16px 18px 20px; background: none;")
rep(".qa-in p { padding: 0 18px 20px 52px; color: #c9cac4;", ".qa-in p { padding: 0 20px 20px; color: #d6d7d2;")
rep(".qa-in p { padding: 0 24px 24px 62px; font-size: 16px; }", ".qa-in p { padding: 0 24px 24px; font-size: 16px; }")
# icon: visible at rest
rep(".qa-i { position: relative; flex: none; width: 34px; height: 34px; border-radius: 50%; border: 1px solid #45464b;",
    ".qa-i { position: relative; flex: none; width: 34px; height: 34px; border-radius: 50%; border: 1px solid #5a5b61; background: #36373d;")
rep("  .qa h3 { font: inherit; }", "  .qa h3 { font: inherit; }")
rep(".qa, .qa::before, .qa-i, .qa-a, .qa-in p { transition: none !important; }", ".qa, .qa-i, .qa-a, .qa-in p { transition: none !important; }")
rep("/* FAQ: one open at a time; height via grid rows 0fr->1fr, icon springs from + to x, answer fades up out of a blur */",
    "/* FAQ: one open at a time; height via grid rows 0fr->1fr, icon springs from + to x, answer fades up out of a blur. Cards are a step brighter than other cards. */")
open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write(s)

p = 'research/decisions.md'
d = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()
d += """| D33b | FAQ look (revises D33) | **More contrast:** FAQ cards one step brighter than the other cards (`#2c2d33`, border `#3c3d43`, icon on its own disc), answer text brighter. **No gradient** in the open card (solid colour + lime border only, neutral shadow). **No numbering.** | Human request. |
"""
open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write(d)
print('ok')
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-10-11 18:09:16

The FAQ changes are live on https://veenos.vercel.app and pushed to GitHub.

- **More contrast:** the FAQ cards are now a step brighter than the other cards, with a clearer border that lightens on hover. The "+" sits on its own small disc so it's visible before you tap, and the answer text is brighter.
- **No gradient:** an open card is a solid colour with a lime border. I also swapped the lime glow under the open card for a plain dark shadow, since it read as part of the same gradient effect.
- **No numbering:** the 01–05 labels are gone, and each answer now lines up with its question.

The opening animation is the same as before. I checked it on phone and desktop, and 16/16 tests pass.

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-10-11 18:17:12

Not entirely. The README and the ads were a few steps behind the final page, while the A/B hypotheses were already accurate. Everything below is now updated, deployed and pushed to GitHub.

**Ads (fixed and re-rendered, images above)**
- **Colours:** the ads and the share image still used the old darker card and border colours. They now use the final page's colours.
- **Glow:** they also had a lime radial glow, which the page no longer has anywhere; I removed it, given you asked to drop the gradient from the FAQ.
- **Destination link:** `ads/README.md` had a placeholder domain. The links now point to https://veenos.vercel.app.
- **Ad/page pairing:** I added a note on how ads and page variants combine. Each ad lands on a random page variant (a 2 × 2 setup), so the creative and the page can be judged separately. Adding `&v=a` or `&v=b` to an ad's link would pin it to one variant.
- **Unchanged:** the numbers, the copy and the "Stejný index. Jiná cena." headline already matched.

**README (fixed)**
- **Section order:** now describes the always-lit compare button with deselect, the counting result animation, the 80 % fact band and the FAQ.
- **Measurement:** FAQ opens are now in the tracked-steps list.
- **Lighthouse:** re-run on the final page. Performance 95, accessibility 100, best practices 100, SEO 100. Load time (LCP) 2.4 s, layout shift (CLS) 0.
- **Testing:** now covers both test e-mails and the edge check across 10 screen widths. Before, it mentioned one e-mail and three widths.
- **AI section:** now 16 decision rounds instead of 13, and 16 tests instead of 15. It also lists the post-build bugs. You spotted the edge and footer problems on screenshots, not the AI. The false Lighthouse layout-shift result came from measuring before the new deploy was live.
- **Google Ads date:** the README said September 2026. Google's own pages give both 23 Jul and 15 Sep 2026, so it now states both.
- **What's missing:** now says the footer contact is fictional and the Pixel is off on purpose. It also flags that counting visits without consent needs a legal check in Czechia.

**A/B hypotheses: no change needed.** All three still describe the final page:
1. Showing part of the result and the rest only after the e-mail.
2. Page variant B (young man + "Spočítat poplatky") against A (Prague Castle + "Začít investovat").
3. Asking for e-mail only, without the name.

All three can be measured with the steps we already track.

The last thing only you can do is run `/export` into `ai-log/`, commit and push it. After that, send the repo (https://github.com/Matush77/Clientelo-task-b) and the site (https://veenos.vercel.app) by the 16 Oct deadline.

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-10-11 18:28:55

The compaction happens at line 2749 of 3894, so the copy will keep lines 1–2744, the full conversation up to just before the compaction. Creating it under a new session ID:

**→ Bash** — Create a pre-compaction copy of the session for export

```
python -I - <<'EOF'
import json, uuid, pathlib
d = pathlib.Path(r'C:\Users\matus\.claude\projects\C--Users-matus-Desktop-B-project')
src = d / '66014a9f-a5a6-413b-8552-f9844a0020a6.jsonl'
lines = src.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines()
b = next(i for i, l in enumerate(lines) if '"subtype":"compact_boundary"' in l)
keep = [l for l in lines[:b] if json.loads(l).get('type') not in ('last-prompt', 'custom-title', 'agent-name', 'atis-latch')]
new = str(uuid.uuid4())
out = []
for l in keep:
    o = json.loads(l)
    if o.get('sessionId') == '66014a9f-a5a6-413b-8552-f9844a0020a6':
        o['sessionId'] = new
    out.append(json.dumps(o, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')))
(d / f'{new}.jsonl').write_text('\n'.join(out) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
last = json.loads(out[-1])
print('new session id:', new, '| lines:', len(out), '| last entry:', last.get('type'), last.get('timestamp'))
EOF
```
